Extensive experiments on memory-intensive simulation benchmarks and real-world tasks show that TTT-RM consistently improves across multiple memory designs, outperforms diverse baselines, and supports sustained execution on a three-minute, eight-stage stowing task.

</details>


### [37] [Emergent Underactuation in Fully Actuated Multirotors by Minimizing a Homogeneous Allocation Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04726)
*Antonio Franchi,Barbara Bazzana*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"对偶原理"：仅规定位置轨迹时，最小化正齐次分配代价会使全驱动多旋翼表现出类欠驱动行为，从而用该代价框架统一解释最优标称运动的欠驱动性与全驱动在扰动抑制中的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全驱动通常被认为有利于独立的位姿跟踪与扰动抑制，但其在仅跟踪位置轨迹时的实际作用机制缺乏统一解释。作者希望回答：为何最优标称运动常呈现"类欠驱动"特征，而全驱动能力又不可替代。

Method: 定义一个正齐次分配代价类（含范数与幂律，覆盖最小范数伪逆分配）。通过"逐纤维下确界化"把执行器-输出代价提升到wrench空间，得到齐次的下确界分配代价；在单位零力矩约束下构造方向性景观，证明其极小方向与力幅值无关。进一步推导最优性间隙的精确分解，以刻画其他姿态轨迹的额外代价及最小代价族。

Result: 将任一固定极小方向与所需世界系力对齐，可同时最小化瞬时与积分的零力矩分配代价，且机体力保持在一维固定子空间内、无横向分量；给出量化任意其他姿态轨迹额外代价的最优性间隙分解。当扰动或预定运动变化改变所需力方向时，全驱动平台可立刻用横向权限生成修改后的力，再转向新的最小代价族；结构欠驱动平台则须先转向才能生成该力。

Conclusion: 齐次分配代价最小化从原理上解释了：最优标称运动为何可以呈现类欠驱动形态（源于代价最小化下的单个固定力方向），而全驱动仍因具备横向权限而在扰动抑制与瞬态力生成中保持不可替代的价值；二者构成互补关系。

Abstract: Full actuation is commonly valued for independent position and attitude tracking. This work establishes a complementary principle: when only a position trajectory is prescribed, minimizing a positively homogeneous allocation cost selects underactuated-like behavior on a fully actuated multirotor. This cost class includes norms and power laws, including standard minimum-norm pseudoinverse allocation. We use fiberwise infimization to transport the actuator-output cost to wrench space, yielding a homogeneous infimal allocation cost. Restriction to unit zero-moment forces defines a directional landscape whose minimizing directions are independent of force magnitude. We show that aligning any fixed minimizing direction with the required world-frame force minimizes instantaneous and integrated zero-moment allocation costs. The resulting body force remains in a fixed one-dimensional subspace without transverse components. We derive an exact optimality-gap decomposition quantifying the additional cost of every other attitude trajectory and characterizing the minimum-cost class. When a disturbance or prescribed-motion change modifies the required-force direction, a fully actuated platform can immediately generate the modified force using transverse authority and reorient toward the new minimum-cost family. A structurally underactuated platform must reorient before generating that force. Thus, homogeneous allocation-cost minimization explains why optimal nominal motion can be underactuated-like, while full actuation remains valuable for disturbance rejection and transient force generation.

</details>


### [38] [Robot Learning with Visual Predicted Force](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04741)
*Haonan Chen,Feiyang Wu,Yuxiang Ma,Mustafa Mete,Pengfei Ye,Junxuan Shen,Cheng Zhu,Aurora Ruggeri,Kelvin Cheung,Jiayuan Mao,Edward Adelson,Jiajun Wu,Robert D. Howe,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出通过柔性Fin Ray夹爪的形变进行视觉力预测，无需力/触觉传感器即可实现力感知操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 力感知操作通常依赖专用力或触觉传感器，这类传感器成本高、部署复杂。作者希望探索是否可通过视觉预测力，从而在部署时摆脱对力/触觉传感器的依赖。

Method: 训练两个模型：首先在标定数据上训练视觉力估计器，并用其为任务演示标注力估计值；其次在力增强的演示数据上训练"动作-力提议"策略，联合生成候选机器人动作及其对应力。测试时采样候选动作及其预期力，选择预测力最接近演示中目标力的动作执行。

Result: 在浆果采摘、空罐抓取、手内重定向和插头插入四个任务上评估，结果表明视觉力预测能在推理阶段指导动作选择，实现接触丰富的操作。

Conclusion: 视觉力预测可引导推理期动作选择，用于接触丰富操作，且部署时无需力或触觉传感器。

Abstract: Force-aware manipulation typically relies on specialized force or tactile sensors. We show that force-aware manipulation can instead be achieved through visual force prediction from the deformation of a compliant Fin Ray gripper. Our approach trains two models. First, we train a visual force estimator on calibration data and use it to annotate task demonstrations with force estimates. Second, we train an action--force proposal policy on these force-augmented demonstrations to jointly generate candidate robot actions and their associated forces. At test time, we sample candidate actions and the forces they are expected to produce, then execute the action whose predicted force is closest to a target from the demonstrations. We evaluate our approach on berry picking, empty-can grasping, in-hand reorientation, and plug insertion. Our results show that visual force prediction can guide inference-time action selection for contact-rich manipulation without requiring force or tactile sensors at deployment.

</details>


### [39] [Lie Rarely, Lie Big: Stealthy Insider Attacks on LLM Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04744)
*Sribalaji C. Anand,George J. Pappas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将受控机器人建模为受团队检测器约束的隐蔽对手，推导出验证概率下界与地图误差上界，并在LLM机器人实验中验证两界成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人团队将规划与互信委托给LLM智能体时，单个被攻陷机器人即可破坏共享结果。既有安全研究多以能量或扰动幅度为约束，而本文关注一种更现实的威胁：对手的隐蔽性不由自身资源决定，而由团队自身的验证机制（带预算的物理世界核验）所限制。这一"验证预算"与任务推进之间存在权衡，尚缺乏系统理论刻画。

Method: 本文以多机器人勘测为具体落地任务：测量可对照物理世界验证，但每次验证消耗本可推进任务的预算。将被攻陷机器人视为系统论意义上的隐蔽对手，其约束来自团队检测器而非能量界。随后推导两个界：一是对手报告被验证的概率下界，以通信图度数与验证预算表示；二是任意隐蔽对手造成的地图误差上界，表示为对手偏差分布上的线性规划最优值，其解给出"隐蔽预算—破坏程度"的交换率。

Result: 理论上，临界验证水平以下，最坏隐蔽攻击是在最不可能被核验的记录上撒罕见的满幅谎言；临界水平以上，更划算的是隐藏在噪声中的小幅偏差。实验中诚实机器人为LLM智能体，两界在实际攻击所花费的预算处均成立。实验还显示，LLM机器人对哪些记录进行复检是无偏的，但复检的数量不可预测。

Conclusion: 单个被攻陷机器人对LLM多机器人团队的共享结果构成可量化的隐蔽威胁，该威胁的上限可由验证预算与通信图结构给出的线性规划刻画，并呈现明确的预算—破坏交换率。LLM机器人的复检位置选择无偏但强度不可预测，这为设计验证预算分配与鲁棒协作机制提供了依据。

Abstract: When a team of robots delegates planning and mutual trust to LLM agents, a single compromised robot can corrupt the shared outcome. We study this threat in a grounded task: a multi-robot survey in which measurements can be verified against the physical world, but every verification costs budget that would otherwise advance the mission. We treat the compromised robot as a stealthy adversary in the system-theoretic sense: it is limited not by an energy bound but by the team's own detectors. We then derive two bounds. First, the probability that the adversary's reports are verified is bounded below in terms of the degrees in the communication graph and the verification budget. Second, the map error caused by any stealthy adversary is bounded above by the value of a linear program over the adversary's bias distributions; its solution is an exchange rate between stealth budget and damage: below a critical verification level the worst stealthy attack tells rare, full-magnitude lies on the records least likely to be verified, and above it the better purchase is small biases hidden in the noise. In experiments where the honest robots are LLM agents, both bounds hold at the budget the attack actually spent. The experiments also show that which records an LLM robot re-checks is unbiased, but how much it re-checks is unpredictable.

</details>


### [40] [DNPush: Distributed Nonparallel Pushing Force Control of Multi-Robot Systems for Object Transportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04764)
*Duy Anh Nguyen,Pham Duy Hung,Trung Dung Ngo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DNPush：一种无需机器人间通信的分布式非平行推力控制器，仅用局部力测量、预设力权重和局部表达的目标方向，通过反正弦律实现协作搬运的收敛与速度定向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作搬运通常依赖机器人间通信、物体模型、接触位置、质心估计或力臂信息，增加了系统复杂度与在线计算负担。本文旨在去除这些依赖，仅凭局部力信息实现分布式非平行推力控制。

Method: 每个机器人使用局部力测量、预设力权重与局部表达的目标搬运方向，无需物体模型、接触位置、质心估计或在线力臂；通过反正弦律由局部力方向失配调节力幅值。在给定接触条件下，用Lyapunov分析与LaSalle不变性原理证明唯一旋转平衡点的收敛性。

Result: 证明垂直力分量在平衡点相互抵消、法向力合力矩为零，物体速度收敛至目标方向，且凸多边形存在合适接触区域。仿真分别隔离验证反正弦缩放机制、五机协作与主动旋转阻尼；软触觉差速驱动机器人硬件实验中，五次试验速度方向误差为0.61°±0.79°，并以三机实现路径点搬运。

Conclusion: DNPush在无通信、无物体模型的条件下实现分布式非平行推力协作搬运，理论收敛性与仿真、硬件实验均验证其有效性。

Abstract: This letter proposes DNPush, a Distributed Nonparallel Pushing force controller for cooperative object transport without inter-robot communication. Each robot uses local force measurements, a preassigned force weight, and a locally expressed desired transport direction, without requiring an object model, contact locations, a CoM estimate, or moment arm magnitudes online. DNPush regulates nonparallel force magnitudes from local force direction misalignments through a reciprocal-sine law. Under the stated contact conditions, Lyapunov analysis with LaSalle's invariance principle proves convergence to a unique rotational equilibrium, where perpendicular force contributions cancel and the net torque from normal forces is zero. Object velocity converges to the desired transport direction, and suitable contact regions exist for convex polygons. Simulations isolate the reciprocal-sine scaling mechanism, five-robot coordination, and active rotational damping. Hardware experiments with soft tactile differential-drive robots achieve a velocity direction error of 0.61 deg +/- 0.79 deg over five trials with a fixed transport direction and demonstrate object transport through waypoints with three robots.

</details>


### [41] [PatternDex: Learning Interaction Patterns to Guide Reinforcement Learning of Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation of Articulated Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04765)
*David Minkwan Kim,Runfa Blark Li,Beckham Po-Ju Lee,Nikolay Atanasov,Truong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PatternDex，通过从人类演示中学习"交互模式"（token序列）来消除具身差异，引导双臂灵巧手高成功率操作铰接物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂灵巧手操作铰接物体时存在具身差异（embodiment gap），导致从人类演示学习到的策略难以直接迁移到目标机器人，成功率低。

Method: 观察到手部运动与物体运动的关联由物体而非手决定，且可从人-物演示中学习。据此将这种关联表示为token序列即"交互模式"；由该模式估计适配目标机器人的腕部运动与接触点，并用这些估计作为引导训练强化学习策略，使策略仅探索目标机器人可执行的动作；新机器人仅需简单微调即可复用所学交互模式。

Result: 在ARCTIC数据集的人类演示上评估：Allegro手平均成功率92.8%，而SOTA基线仅52.2%；另三种机器人手仅靠微调即达70%以上成功率；策略可迁移到真实世界开微波炉任务。

Conclusion: 将手-物运动关联抽象为与具身无关的交互模式，可有效消除具身差异，实现高成功率且易迁移的双臂灵巧操作。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a method that enables bimanual dexterous hands to manipulate articulated objects with a high success rate without suffering from an embodiment gap. We observe that the correlation between hand motions and object motions is dictated by the object rather than the hands and can be learned from human-object demonstrations. Based on this observation, we propose PatternDex, a method that learns this correlation and represents it as a token sequence, which we call an interaction pattern. From this pattern, PatternDex estimates the wrist motions and contact points that fit the target robot, and then trains a reinforcement learning policy that exploits these estimates as guidance. Since the guidance fits the target embodiment, the policy explores only the actions that the target robot can execute and thus achieves high success rates. PatternDex also requires only simple fine-tuning to train a new robot, since it can reuse the learned interaction pattern. We evaluate PatternDex with bimanual dexterous hands on human demonstrations from the ARCTIC dataset. PatternDex achieves, on average, a 92.8% success rate with Allegro hands, while the state-of-the-art baseline achieves 52.2%. Also, it achieves success rates above 70% with three other robot hands after fine-tuning alone. Furthermore, we verify that the learned policy transfers well to a real-world task of opening a microwave. Videos and additional results are available at https://patterndex.github.io/PatternDex/

</details>


### [42] [Flow Policies as Actions of Skill-Level World Models: Learned and Symbolic Abstractions for Long-Horizon Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04767)
*Andreu Matoses Gimenez,Andrei-Carlo Papuc,Chris Pek,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 利用流匹配策略构建技能级动作抽象，使一次技能执行为一次世界模型转移，并在积木重排任务上评估四种动作抽象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制率动作的长程规划需要大量预测步，搜索空间大且误差累积；而符号技能词表依赖领域知识与标注示范。

Method: 在分段为完整技能的示范上训练流匹配策略，将噪声种子与观测（可选码或标签）映射为完整技能执行；据此提出四种递增任务知识的动作抽象：压缩种子、两种从示范学习的离散码、符号标签；用统一的世界模型训练与规划框架评估。

Result: 在需多达14个连续技能的仿真积木重排任务中，符号标签在不超过六个技能的任务上成功率超90%，之后下降；无标签时，以物体为中心的学习码在单技能任务上与之相当，在二至五技能任务上保持其成功率的二分之一至四分之三。消融显示标签优势多来自规划器知道哪些动作可用，而非标签本身。超过六个技能时，限制成功的是搜索而非世界模型。

Conclusion: 技能级动作抽象可缩短规划序列、降低误差累积；无标签学习码在中等长度任务上是有竞争力的替代；长程瓶颈主要在搜索而非世界模型。

Abstract: Latent world models enable robots to plan by predicting the consequences of actions. Planning long tasks with control-rate actions requires many prediction steps, which enlarges the search space and accumulates error. Skill-level actions shorten these sequences, but a symbolic skill vocabulary requires domain knowledge and labeled demonstrations. We construct skill-level actions from the inputs of a flow-matching policy trained on demonstrations segmented into complete skills. The policy maps a noise seed and an observation, optionally with a code or label, to a complete skill execution, so one execution is one world-model transition. On this mechanism we propose four action abstractions with increasing task knowledge: a compressed seed, two discrete codes learned from the demonstrations, and a symbolic label. We evaluate them with a common world-model training procedure and planning framework on simulated block rearrangement tasks that require up to 14 sequential skills. The symbolic label succeeds in over 90% of the tasks that require up to six skills and degrades beyond. Without any label, an object-centric learned code matches it on single-skill tasks and retains half to three quarters of its success on tasks of two to five skills. Ablations attribute much of the label's advantage to its planner knowing which actions are applicable, rather than to the label itself. Beyond six skills the search, not the world model, limits success. Project page: https://andreumatoses.github.io/research/flow-skill-wm

</details>


### [43] [Learning Task and Motion Plans from Real Demonstrations with Hybrid Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04771)
*Zuleika Redondo Garcia,Andreu Matoses Gimenez,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出混合流匹配规划器，用单一网络同时生成符号计划（掩码离散流匹配）与运动轨迹（连续流匹配），可在少量演示下学习并闭环执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程移动操作需要任务计划与执行动作，现有生成式规划器依赖数千条固定基座机械臂的脚本演示且只能开环执行，数据需求大、鲁棒性差。

Method: 采用混合流匹配：单一网络以掩码离散流匹配生成符号计划、以连续流匹配生成运动轨迹；利用两条数据特性补偿小数据集——从中间动作续接的演示本身即新演示（扩充样本并支持每步重规划），同类物体可互换（把单一目标演示转化为所有置换目标的演示）；并提出离散计划的训练与生成方案。

Result: 基础实现在68%的留出场景生成有效计划，训练与生成方案提升至76%；每步重规划使运动学仿真中任务完成率从40%升至53%；任务完成率与仅运动的流匹配策略相当且额外提供符号计划，在完成率与计划有效性上均优于已有混合扩散方案，并在真实移动机械臂上完成验证。

Conclusion: 混合流匹配规划器能在少量演示、闭环执行条件下统一生成符号计划与轨迹，性能优于现有生成式与混合扩散规划器，并具备真实机器人可行性。

Abstract: Long-horizon mobile manipulation requires a task plan and the motion that executes it. Generative planners trained on demonstration produce both in one pass, requiring neither a symbolic domain nor search. To date, however, they have relied on thousands of scripted demonstrations of fixed-base arms and executed open loop. This paper presents a hybrid flow matching planner: a single network generates the symbolic plan with masked discrete flow matching and the motion trajectory with continuous flow matching. Unlike prior generative planners, we aim to learn from a much smaller set of demonstrations and to execute the plan in closed loop. Two properties of the data compensate for the small dataset. A demonstration resumed from any of its intermediate actions is itself a demonstration, which multiplies the training samples and enables replanning after every action. Objects of the same kind are interchangeable, which turns demonstrations of one goal into demonstrations of every permuted goal. Our base implementation produces valid plans on 68% of held-out scenes; a training and generation scheme for the discrete plan raises this to 76%, and replanning after every action raises the task completion rate from 40% to 53% in a kinematic simulation. The planner matches the task completion rate of motion-only flow matching policies while additionally providing the symbolic plan, and it outperforms previous hybrid diffusion formulations on both task completion and plan validity. We validate the planner on a real mobile manipulator. Videos and project page: https://andreumatoses.github.io/research/hybrid-flow-planning

</details>


### [44] [Distributed Cascade Force Control of Soft-Tactile-Based Multi-robot System for Object Transportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04775)
*Duy Anh Nguyen,Nhat Minh Dinh Le,Nhan Huu Nguyen,Pham Duy Hung,Van Anh Ho,Trung Dung Ngo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种分布式级联力控系统（DCFC），使多台配备360度视觉软触觉传感器的机器人无需机器人间通信即可将刚性物体推向移动目标，并通过李雅普诺夫理论及仿真与实物实验验证其稳定性与协调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多机器人推动刚性物体朝向移动目标的任务中，机器人间通信往往受限或不可用，且物体质量未知，因此需要一种无需机器人间通信、对未知物体质量具有鲁棒性的分布式协同控制方法。

Method: 提出分布式级联力控系统（DCFC）。机器人搭载360度视觉软触觉传感器，用于确定接触位置与合力。基于平面上移动刚性物体的动力学分析，设计分布式级联控制：内环控制融合接触力与定位，确保机器人维持推压接触并向物体施加期望力；外环控制在无机器人间通信条件下协调多机器人沿期望方向推动物体，且不依赖已知物体质量。采用李雅普诺夫稳定性理论验证系统的稳定性与收敛性。

Result: 通过仿真与真实世界实验验证了所提控制方法的性能，实验结果展示了多机器人在将物体推向移动的期望方向时能够成功协调。

Conclusion: 所提出的DCFC方法可在无机器人间通信、物体质量未知的条件下实现多机器人对刚性物体的协同推动，并具备理论保证的稳定性与收敛性，仿真与实物实验均验证了其有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a distributed cascade force control system (DCFC) for multiple robots with the aim of pushing a rigid object towards a desired moving target without their inter-robot communication. These mobile robots are equipped with 360-degree vision-based soft tactile sensors utilized to determine contact location and resultant impact force. By investigating the dynamics of moving rigid objects on the flat, we proposed a distributed cascade control. The inner loop control incorporates contact force and positioning, ensuring the robots' pushing contact and application of the desired force to the object. The outer loop control coordinates the robots to push the object in a desired direction without inter-robot communication, regardless of the unknown object mass. The stability and convergence of the system are verified using the Lyapunov stability theory. We also conducted simulation and real-word experiments to validate the performance of the proposed control method, and the experimental results showcase the successful coordination of multiple robots in pushing an object towards a moving desired direction.

</details>


### [45] [ExStereo: Lifting 2D Vision-Language-Action Models to 3D with Explicit Stereo Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04805)
*I-Chun Arthur Liu,Jason Chen,Gaurav S. Sukhatme,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ExStereo：用立体匹配基础模型为2D VLA注入3D感知，通过立体表示、动作-立体交叉注意力和自监督中期训练，在仿真与真实双臂平台上稳定提升操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人高精度操作依赖三维感知，但从单目RGB恢复度量深度与精确三维位置本质上是病态问题；而现有Vision-Language-Action（VLA）模型多仅依赖RGB观测，缺乏三维感知能力。

Method: 提出ExStereo立体模块：借助立体匹配基础模型，从立体图像对重建场景几何，并将多视角观测渲染为显式立体表示以提取立体特征；通过动作-立体交叉注意力机制，使动作专家的动作token选择性关注立体token，从而生成以三维场景信息为条件的动作。训练上引入任务特定后训练之前的"中期训练"阶段，在大规模立体数据上使用自监督学习目标学习鲁棒的3D表征。

Result: 在公开VLA模型π_{0.5}与SmolVLA上进行微调，并在仿真环境及真实世界双臂PiPER平台上评估，经ExStereo微调的VLA在两种设置下均持续优于基线。

Conclusion: 立体感知能有效增强VLA的三维场景理解，从而提升机器人操作性能；该模块可即插即用地为预训练2D VLA补充3D感知能力。

Abstract: Three-dimensional perception is critical for robotic manipulation, particularly for high-precision tasks, as recovering metric depth and precise 3D object positions from monocular RGB observations is inherently ill-posed. However, many Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models rely solely on RGB observations for perception. Leveraging recent advances in foundation models for stereo matching, we introduce ExStereo, a stereo module that augments pre-trained 2D VLAs with 3D perception. ExStereo reconstructs scene geometry from stereo image pairs and renders multi-view observations as an explicit stereo representation for stereo feature extraction. The action tokens from the action expert selectively attend to the resulting stereo tokens through our proposed action-stereo cross-attention mechanism, enabling the policy to generate robot actions conditioned on 3D scene information. To learn robust 3D representations, we introduce a mid-training stage before task-specific post-training, using a self-supervised learning objective on large-scale stereo data. We validate our approach by fine-tuning two publicly available VLAs, $π_{0.5}$ and SmolVLA, and evaluate them in simulation and on a real-world bimanual PiPER platform. Across both settings, VLAs fine-tuned with ExStereo consistently outperform baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of stereo perception for robotic manipulation. Our project website is at: https://exstereo-vla.github.io/ExStereo/.

</details>


### [46] [Tackling Sim-to-Real Mismatch Through Sampling-Based Disturbance Observers: From Analytical Models to Learned World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04896)
*Tianqi Zhu,Jun Yang,Jianliang Mao,Cong Li,Shihua Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于采样的扰动观测器（SDOB），将扰动观测器原理扩展到仿真器和学习世界模型，通过状态推演或代价查询接口估计并补偿扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人控制器日益依赖解析模型、仿真器、代价查询接口和学习世界模型，但物理部署常偏离名义假设，且即使模型本身准确也可能出现额外扰动。经典扰动观测器（DOB）通常依赖显式被控对象模型，难以适用于仿真器与学习模型等更广泛的模型形式。

Method: 提出基于采样的扰动观测器（SDOB），将DOB原理扩展到包括仿真器和学习世界模型在内的更广泛模型，通过状态推演（state-rollout）或代价查询（cost-query）接口实现。SDOB区分两个可观测通道：状态效应扰动（反馈状态与其预测不一致）和代价扰动（同一查询状态因感知环境变化而得到不同代价）。

Result: 在传统模型与学习模型上开展了多样化的仿真与真实机器人实验，结果表明SDOB能够有效补偿仿真到现实的失配（sim-to-real mismatch）并提升控制性能。

Conclusion: SDOB将扰动观测思想推广至更广泛的模型接口，为仿真器与学习世界模型下的扰动估计与补偿提供了可行方案，并在实验中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Robotic controllers increasingly rely on analytical models, simulators, cost-query interfaces, and learned world models. However, physical deployment can deviate from nominal assumptions, and additional disturbances may arise even when the model itself is accurate. In control systems, disturbance observers (DOB) are widely used to estimate such unmeasured effects from nominal models and measured feedback. Classical DOB formulations are generally built around explicit plant models. This paper develops the sampling-based disturbance observer (SDOB), extending the DOB principle to a broader range of models, including simulators and learned world models, through state-rollout or cost-query interfaces. SDOB separates two observable channels: state-effect disturbances, for which the feedback state differs from its prediction, and cost disturbances, for which the same query state receives different costs as the perceived environment changes. Diverse simulation and real-robot experiments across traditional and learned models demonstrate the effectiveness of SDOB in compensating for sim-to-real mismatch and improving control performance.

</details>


### [47] [GJK-CBF: Control Barrier Functions for Convex Rigid Body Collision Avoidance on SE(3)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04910)
*Yi-Hsuan Chen,Shuo Liu,Wei Xiao,Michael Otte,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GJK-CBF，利用GJK算法获取凸体间的witness pair，直接由刚体相对运动构造CBF梯度，避免可微优化开销，在2D/3D多场景实现无碰撞运动并降低保守性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸体间避碰是机器人学基础问题。CBF因计算高效成为实时安全滤波的实用框架，但一般凸体的精确分离度量（距离或缩放因子）通常需通过优化求解，现有CBF公式多依赖可微优化（diffOpt）获取梯度，带来额外计算开销。因此需要一种无需diffOpt、计算更高效的CBF构造方法。

Method: 利用Gilbert-Johnson-Keerthi（GJK）算法获取当前witness pair——即实现最小距离或穿透深度的一对点，据此构造称为GJK-CBF的控制障碍函数，其梯度直接由witness pair的相对刚体运动构造，不依赖可微优化。该方法适用于2D与3D环境下的广泛凸体对，只要每对交互中至少有一个是严格凸体。

Result: GJK-CBF在多种场景中得到验证，包括2D和3D的多机器人位置互换、3D中穿越垂直狭缝，以及机械臂的应用。结果显示所有场景均实现无碰撞运动，同时降低了由几何近似引入的保守性，尤其在狭窄环境中效果明显。

Conclusion: GJK-CBF通过GJK获取witness pair并直接构造梯度，规避了diffOpt的计算开销，在2D/3D多种凸体交互场景下实现无碰撞运动并减少保守性，为凸体避碰提供了一种高效实用的CBF方案。

Abstract: Collision avoidance among convex bodies is a fundamental problem in robotics. Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) provide a practical framework for real-time safety filtering due to their computational efficiency. For general convex bodies, exact separation measures, such as distance or scaling factor, are typically computed through optimization. Existing CBF formulations often obtain the required gradient via differentiable optimization (diffOpt), adding computational overhead. In contrast, we leverage the Gilbert-Johnson-Keerthi (GJK) algorithm to obtain the current witness pair---the pair of points realizing the minimum distance or penetration depth---and formulate a CBF, termed GJK-CBF, whose gradient is constructed directly from the relative rigid-body motion of the witness pair, without resorting to diffOpt. This formulation applies to both 2D and 3D environments across a broad class of convex body pairs, provided that at least one in each one-to-one interaction is strictly convex. The proposed GJK-CBF is validated in various scenarios, including multi-robot position swapping in both 2D and 3D, navigation through a vertical slit in 3D, and its applicability to manipulators. The results demonstrate collision-free motion across all scenarios while reducing the conservativeness introduced by geometric approximations, particularly in narrow environments.

</details>


### [48] [$R^2$-WAM: Repair-and-Reject Post-Training for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04913)
*Ruiyan Xu,Haisheng Su,Sixu Lin,Zhaokun Yue,Chengming Hu,Xin Jin,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出R²-WAM两阶段"修复-拒绝"后训练框架，通过运动学对齐提升世界动作模型预测与输入动作的一致性，并用修复后的未来预测进行负样本微调，在仿真与真实任务上取得高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）通过预测采样动作的后果来精炼策略，但视觉上合理的预测可能无法真实反映输入动作，从而误导策略精炼。核心问题是预测未来与输入动作之间的不一致。

Method: 两阶段后训练框架：修复阶段引入运动学对齐分数，衡量预测运动与示教运动的一致性，使预测视频忠实反映输入动作；拒绝阶段利用修复后的视频模型比较采样动作与示教动作的想象结果，对预测任务进度低于示教参考且超过规定裕度的样本，选择性施加负向微调。无需额外环境交互，也不改变推理流程。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0的干净与随机化设置下平均成功率93.8%；在长时程真实世界折叠衬衫（Fold Shirt）任务上平均成功率87.5%，而Fast-WAM为0%。

Conclusion: 将视频预测从表征学习拓展到基于后果的策略精炼，验证了修复-拒绝两阶段设计的有效性，显著提升策略精炼的可靠性与任务成功率。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) emerge as a promising foundation for policy refinement by predicting the consequences of sampled actions. However, visually plausible predictions can mislead policy refinement if they fail to reflect the input actions. To address this mismatch, we introduce $R^2$-WAM, a two-stage repair-and-reject post-training framework that first improves the consistency of predicted futures with input actions, then uses these futures to select inferior action samples for negative fine-tuning. The repair stage grounds imagination in observed robot behavior through a kinematic alignment score that measures agreement between predicted and demonstrated motion, enabling the predicted video to faithfully reflect its input actions. Using the repaired video model, the rejection stage compares imagined outcomes of sampled and demonstrated actions, selectively applying negative fine-tuning to samples whose predicted task progress falls below the demonstrated reference by a prescribed margin. Together, the two stages extend video prediction from representation learning to consequence-based policy refinement without additional environment interaction or changes to the inference procedure. $R^2$-WAM achieves 93.8% average success on RoboTwin 2.0 across clean and randomized settings. On the long-horizon real-world Fold Shirt task, it achieves 87.5% average success, compared with 0% for Fast-WAM.

</details>


### [49] [PreAct-Nav: Agentic Reasoning Before Action for Urban Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04916)
*Jing Xie,Shouwei Ruan,Yubin Wang,Yuxiang Zhang,Junwei Yang,Songchang Jin,Dianxi Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PreAct-Nav，通过中期子目标锚定、动作条件世界模型预测与记忆更新，为冻结策略赋予前瞻性推理能力，实现大规模城市具身导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市导航要求具身智能体基于自我中心观测进行长时程决策。现有方法依赖对瞬时观测或有限历史的语言推理，难以将远端目标转化为连贯的局部决策，且缺乏对动作后果与未来状态的前瞻能力，在长距离、多转弯的城市路线中表现受限。

Method: 以持久的中期子目标锚定局部决策：1）导航记忆模块维护当前活动子目标与相关经验，将远端目标转化为可执行的中间目标；2）预测性世界沙盒利用动作条件世界模型（AC-WM）在候选动作下预测世界动态；3）VLM 推理器在当前子目标下解读预测，决定保留或修正动作；4）执行后用真实观测评估结果、纠正不一致假设并更新记忆，以延续或重构子目标。整体为冻结策略提供前瞻推理框架。

Result: 大量实验表明，PreAct-Nav 通过记忆更新与视觉预测改善了动作选择；在更长路线和转弯更多的路线上增益尤为明显。

Conclusion: 将局部决策锚定于持续的中期子目标、在动作执行前预测其后果，并结合真实反馈迭代更新推理上下文，可有效提升大规模城市环境中的长时程具身导航性能，且对复杂长路线收益更大。

Abstract: Urban navigation requires embodied agents to pursue long-horizon goals through local decisions based on egocentric observations. However, existing agentic navigation methods often struggle to translate distant goals into coherent local decisions in large-scale physical environments. Their reliance on linguistic reasoning over transient observations or limited history constrains anticipation of the consequences of actions and future conditions, despite the importance of such foresight for navigating long and complex urban routes. To bridge this gap, we propose PreAct-Nav, an agentic navigation framework that equips frozen policies with anticipatory reasoning for robust urban navigation. Our central idea is to anchor local decisions in persistent medium-horizon subgoals, assess the consequences of predicted actions before execution, and continually update the reasoning context using actual outcomes. At its core, a navigation memory module maintains the active subgoal and relevant experience across decisions, translating distant goals into actionable intermediate objectives. We further introduce a predictive world sandbox that uses an action-conditioned world model (AC-WM) to forecast world dynamics conditioned on candidate movements. A vision-language model (VLM) reasoner interprets these predictions under the current subgoal to retain or revise actions. After execution, real observations are used to assess outcomes, correct inconsistent assumptions, and update memory to continue or reformulate the subgoal. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that the proposed PreAct-Nav improves action selection through memory updates and visual prediction, with more pronounced gains on longer routes and routes with more turns.

</details>


### [50] [Nudge Before You Push: Physics-Aware Navigation via Tactile Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04924)
*Xianyao Li,Fang Xu,Ruitong Tian,Bowen Sun,Xiao Hu,Yang Ye,Jing Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TANav系统，通过短暂轻推测量容器的推动阻力以推断载荷质量，配合TacPhys力序列学习模型与重复巡逻规划器，实现安全高效的导航决策，并在仿真与四足机械臂上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉上完全相同的容器可能装载不同质量的物体，其搬运决策也不同；仅靠视觉（RGB-D）难以区分这些“隐藏载荷”，容易造成误推或过度绕行。

Method: TANav通过一次短暂轻推测量推动阻力，在站点定义的操作边界内进行导航；TacPhys将力序列（可选融合RGB-D与运动学信息）映射为质量估计以授权推动；重复巡逻规划器权衡探测成本与路径成本，必要时请求第二次接触，并跨访问复用观测。

Result: 仿真中TacPhys接近仅依赖阻力的贝叶斯参考，在相当的低风险工作点上将漏推率从峰值力阈值法的28.7%降至5.5%；重复巡逻仿真中TANav恢复oracle路径节省的90%，人工干预比始终绕行减少一半以上，边界违规从仅RGB-D探测的4.3%降至2.9%；四足机械臂配霍尔阵列指尖上，离线零样本MAE为0.28–1.07 kg（容器≤2.82 kg），3 kg力上升标定离线汇总准确率93.5%（非立方体场景86.4%），独立原始峰值规则在未见箱体上20次在线决策中正确15次。

Conclusion: 通过主动轻推感知载荷阻力并结合学习式物理推理与巡逻规划，TANav能在视觉不可区分的情况下提升导航决策的安全性与效率，且具备向真实机器人平台迁移的可行性。

Abstract: Visually identical containers can conceal loads that require different handling decisions. We present TANav, which uses a brief nudge to measure push resistance for navigation under a site-defined handling boundary. TacPhys reads the force sequence, with optional RGB-D and kinematics, into a mass estimate for push authorization. A repeated-patrol planner weighs probe and route costs, requests a second contact when useful, and reuses observations across visits. In simulation, TacPhys approaches a resistance-only Bayes reference and reduces missed pushes from 28.7% to 5.5% relative to peak-force thresholding at comparable low-risk operating points. In repeated-patrol simulation, TANav recovers 90% of the oracle's path saving, more than halves human interventions relative to always-detour, and reduces boundary violations from 4.3% to 2.9% relative to RGB-D-only probing. On a quadruped manipulator with a Hall-array fingertip, offline zero-shot MAE is 0.28-1.07 kg on containers up to 2.82 kg. Force-rise calibration at 3 kg gives 93.5% pooled offline accuracy (86.4% on non-cube episodes); a separate raw-peak rule gives 15 of 20 correct online decisions on unseen boxes.

</details>


### [51] [RobotUse: Allocating Computation, Context, and Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04929)
*Junhoo Lee,Injun Baek,Seungyeon Kim,Suhyun Jeon,Minkyu Kim,Baekseung Kim,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RobotUse 是一个机器人智能体执行框架（harness），将计算、上下文与决策围绕物理动作的指定与修正进行组织，在 RoboLab 上取得 45% 任务成功率，比 CaP-X 高 6.7 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人智能体需要把预期动作与观测结果对应起来，并保留可复用的上下文以在多次尝试中修正决策。现有接口要么把动作选择封装在预定义工具内部，要么要求智能体自行管理细粒度执行代码及其不断膨胀的历史记录，限制了灵活性与可扩展性。

Method: 提出 RobotUse 框架：智能体仅通过视觉方式选择目标与位姿，几何计算、运动规划与控制由后端处理；子智能体（subagents）在每个子目标内保留详细交互，并只回传后续决策所需信息；通过"持续编排"（continual harnessing）从执行中学习，更新持久化的 playbook。

Result: 在 RoboLab 上任务成功率达 45%，超过 CaP-X 6.7 个百分点，同时保持紧凑的决策上下文并减少对预定义动作抽象的依赖；此外证明该框架可在反馈不完美的真实世界执行中学习，并将所学迁移到后续任务。

Conclusion: 以"动作指定—修正"为中心组织计算与上下文的框架设计，可在保持决策上下文紧凑的同时提升机器人任务成功率，并支持从真实执行中持续学习与跨任务迁移，为机器人智能体提供了一种更可扩展的接口范式。

Abstract: Robot agents must connect their intended actions to observed outcomes while retaining the context needed to revise their choices over repeated attempts. Existing interfaces often leave these choices inside predefined tools or require agents to manage detailed execution code and its growing history. We introduce RobotUse, a robot agent harness that organizes computation, context, and decisions around specifying and revising physical actions. Agents visually select targets and poses, while the backend handles geometry, motion planning, and control. Subagents retain detailed interactions within each subgoal and return the information needed for subsequent decisions. Continual harnessing lets agents learn from execution by updating a persistent playbook. On RoboLab, RobotUse achieves 45% task success, outperforming CaP-X by 6.7 percentage points while maintaining compact decision contexts and reducing reliance on predefined action abstractions. Furthermore, we show that RobotUse learns from real-world execution despite imperfect feedback and transfers what it learns to subsequent tasks. Project page is available at https://robotuse-team.github.io/.

</details>


### [52] [DiVeR: Decision-Critical Verifier Learning for VLA Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04933)
*Seongheon Park,Heecheol Kim,Shulin Tian,Lilika Makabe,Namiko Saito,Katsushi Ikeuchi,Sharon Li,Yasuyuki Matsushita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiVeR通过采样动作表示的离散度估计决策关键性，以此重加权验证器学习，聚焦于对动作选择最关键的稀疏状态，从而提升VLA策略的验证器引导测试时扩展效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略受益于数据与模型规模的扩展，但受限于机器人数据的高昂成本。验证器引导的测试时扩展通过采样多个候选动作并选择最可能成功的一个，提供高效替代方案。然而，现有基于分类的验证器虽从轨迹级结果学习，却对所有访问状态一视同仁，忽略了不同状态对候选动作区分的价值差异：许多状态下的合理动作相似、区分信号有限，只有稀疏的决策关键状态才存在能显著影响结果的差异化动作。

Method: 提出DiVeR，从采样动作表示的离散度（dispersion）估计决策关键性，并利用该信号对验证器学习进行重加权，使其偏向动作选择最具后果的状态。该方法无需步级标注或额外的环境交互。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboCasa基准以及Franka Research 3机器人的真实世界实验中，DiVeR通过更有效的验证器引导动作选择，持续提升任务成功率，且验证器推理开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 通过以决策关键性重加权验证器学习，DiVeR在无需额外标注或交互的前提下有效提升了验证器引导的测试时扩展性能，为VLA策略的高效扩展提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Scaling robot data and model capacity has improved Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies, but further progress is constrained by the high cost of robotic data. Verifier-guided test-time scaling offers an efficient alternative by sampling multiple action candidates and selecting the one most likely to lead to task success at inference time. Existing classification-based verifiers learn from trajectory-level outcomes but treat all visited states equally, even though their value for candidate discrimination can vary across a trajectory. At many states, plausible actions are similar and provide limited discrimination signal, while only a sparse set of decision-critical states admits meaningfully different actions that can substantially affect downstream outcomes. To address this, we propose DiVeR, which estimates decision criticality from the dispersion of sampled action representations. DiVeR then uses this signal to reweight verifier learning toward states where action selection is most consequential, without requiring step-level annotations or additional environment interaction. Across LIBERO, RoboCasa, and real-world experiments on a Franka Research 3 robot, DiVeR consistently improves task success through more effective verifier-guided action selection, while adding negligible verifier inference overhead.

</details>


### [53] [A Unified Dynamics Framework for Reinforcement Learning and Classical Control of a Six-DOF Pipeline-Tracking ROV in NVIDIA Isaac Sim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04949)
*Cheng Siong Chin,M. Venkateshkumar,Jianhua Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种六自由度ROV管线跟踪架构：同一USD场景为NumPy版Fossen方程与Isaac Sim中相同的PhysX力模型提供一致参数，统一训练与部署；对PPO、SAC、TD3、DDPG、TRPO及六种经典控制器进行同环境、同奖励、同评估框架的对比，并进行科里奥利项消融。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下航行器强化学习控制器常以某一物理表示训练、却在另一表示上部署，导致报告性能无法反映训练外行为；需要消除"训练-部署"物理不一致，并在统一条件下公平比较学习型与经典控制方法。

Method: 1) 用单一USD场景向两条分支提供BlueROV2-Heavy的质量、附加质量、阻尼、浮力与推力器参数：向量化NumPy实现的Fossen海洋航行器方程，以及NVIDIA Isaac Sim中以相同方程逐步施加为PhysX力的交互式部署。2) 以科里奥利-向心项作为两分支的主导动力学模型，在PPO与TRPO上做受控消融。3) 通过检查点兼容层，用同一评估代码与随机化评估框架训练并评分PPO、SAC、TD3、DDPG、TRPO。4) 扩展六种经典基线（PID、滑模、模糊逻辑、反馈线性化、模型预测控制、自适应神经模糊推理系统），与学习策略共用制导几何与推力分配。

Result: 科里奥利项消融：纳入该项不会使PPO或TRPO失稳，且小幅提升跟踪性能，TRPO的站位RMS误差约降低19%。在启用科里奥利动力学的条件下，PPO、TRPO与反馈线性化取得100%成功率与较强精度的最佳组合；PID、模糊控制与神经模糊基线的成功率亦达100%，但跟踪更宽松；DDPG与TD3各自出现特定且可解释的失败模式，而非离策略学习的一般性缺陷；经典控制仍是对抗最优学习策略的强基线。

Conclusion: 统一的USD参数与方程驱动的训练-部署架构可保证物理一致性；科里奥利项可安全纳入并带来收益；最强学习策略（PPO、TRPO）与反馈线性化并列领先，经典控制仍是强基线，离策略算法的失败具有可解释的个案特性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning controllers for underwater vehicles are usually trained against one physics representation and deployed against another, so reported performance does not always describe behavior outside training. This paper presents a pipeline-tracking architecture for a six-degree-of-freedom remotely operated vehicle (ROV) in which one Universal Scene Description (USD) scene supplies the real BlueROV2-Heavy mass, added-mass, damping, buoyancy, and thruster parameters to both halves of the system: a vectorized NumPy implementation of Fossen's marine-craft equations, and an interactive NVIDIA Isaac Sim deployment applying the identical equations as PhysX forces at every step. The Coriolis-centripetal term is the primary dynamics model in both branches; a controlled ablation on PPO and TRPO shows that including it does not destabilize either algorithm and modestly improves tracking, about 19 percent lower standoff RMS error for TRPO. Five reinforcement learning algorithms, PPO, soft actor-critic, TD3, DDPG, and TRPO, are trained against one environment, reward, and randomized evaluation harness through a checkpoint-compatibility layer scoring any policy with the same code. The pipeline is extended with six classical baselines, PID, sliding-mode, fuzzy logic, feedback linearization, model predictive control, and an adaptive neuro-fuzzy inference system, driven by the same guidance geometry and thruster allocation as the learned policies. Under Coriolis-enabled dynamics, PPO, TRPO, and feedback linearization reach the strongest combination of 100 percent success and competitive accuracy; PID, fuzzy control, and the neuro-fuzzy baseline also reach 100 percent success with looser tracking; DDPG and TD3 each show a specific, explainable failure mode rather than a general weakness of off-policy learning; and classical control remains a strong baseline against the best learned policies.

</details>


### [54] [FreeLoc: Online Floorplan Localization via Diffusion-Aided Pose Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05011)
*Haocheng Peng,Boyang Zhou,Jiarui Hu,Xiyue Guo,Ziyang Zhang,Boming Zhao,Yifan Gao,Xiao Li,Hujun Bao,Zhaopeng Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FreeLoc：将楼层平面图作为可直接查询的几何地图，无需构建离线数据库，实现在线RGB室内定位，单帧与序列定位均达SOTA且可实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高性能楼层平面图定位方法需将平面图转换为密集的、场景专用的离线数据库，导致精度、存储与运行时间都被离散化位姿空间的采样分辨率所绑定，限制了可扩展性与部署灵活性。

Method: 将楼层平面图视为可直接查询的几何地图；提出高效的在线几何查询与扩散辅助精化方案：通过即时平面图射线查询获取合理的位姿锚点，并精化为连续的精确位姿估计；针对序列定位，提出在线似然构建策略，由粗采样候选与精化位姿假设构建似然，衔接单帧定位与概率时序融合，从而支持基于直方图滤波的时序融合而无需离线数据库。

Result: 实验表明方法可实现实时在线推理，并在单帧与序列定位上均取得最先进性能；真实世界实验结果验证了其在室内机器人定位场景中的实际可部署性。

Conclusion: FreeLoc通过直接查询楼层平面图并配合扩散精化与在线似然构建，摆脱了对密集离线数据库的依赖，兼顾精度、存储与实时性，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Floorplans provide compact and widely available geometric maps for indoor localization, but existing high-performing floorplan-based methods still convert them into dense scene-specific offline databases, tying accuracy, storage, and runtime to the sampling resolution of the discretized pose space. We present FreeLoc, an online RGB-based floorplan localization framework that treats the floorplan as a directly queryable geometric map. FreeLoc introduces an efficient online geometric querying and diffusion-aided refinement scheme, which retrieves plausible pose anchors through on-the-fly floorplan ray querying and refines them into accurate continuous pose estimates. For sequential localization, FreeLoc develops an online likelihood construction strategy that bridges single-frame localization and probabilistic temporal fusion by constructing likelihoods from coarse-sampled candidates and refined pose hypotheses, enabling histogram-filter-based temporal fusion without offline databases. Experiments demonstrate real-time online inference and state-of-the-art performance in both single-frame and sequential localization, while real-world results validate practical deployability in indoor robotic localization scenarios.

</details>


### [55] [Now You Feel It, Now You See Me: Digital-Twin-based Teleoperation Interface for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05081)
*Youngchan Shim,Kyutae Lee,JooYun Kim,Jaeseong Hwang,Harim Ji,Yongseok Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于数字孪生的AR遥操作界面，融合裸手追踪、双臂机器人手与虚拟对象表征，通过力可视化与遮挡缓解视觉反馈克服无力反馈和遮挡问题，并经用户实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作是采集高质量示教数据以训练机器人灵巧操作技能的重要手段，而裸手追踪无需手套或外骨骼即可控制机器人手并演示协调手指运动。但该类遥操作存在两大反馈局限：一是缺乏力反馈，操作者只能凭视觉推断接触力，难以精确、安全操作；二是被物体或其他机器人部件遮挡区域缺乏视觉反馈，影响手部位置、接近距离及适配物体形状的预抓取姿态判断。

Method: 构建基于数字孪生的增强现实（AR）遥操作界面，集成裸手追踪、双臂机器人手以及机器人与任务物体的虚拟表征。界面将触觉测量值渲染于机器人手网格上作为视-力反馈；同时提供用户可控的虚拟视角面板或遮挡感知透明化渲染，以实现缓解遮挡的视觉反馈。

Result: 通过包含任务1与任务2的用户研究评估该界面，考察力可视化与视觉辅助如何在需要精细力调节及遮挡条件下操作的任务中支持示教数据采集。

Conclusion: 该数字孪生AR遥操作界面通过视-力反馈与遮挡缓解视觉反馈，针对性地弥补裸手遥操作缺乏力反馈和遮挡视觉信息的两大缺陷，为灵巧操作示教采集提供可行方案，具体效果由用户研究评估。

Abstract: Teleoperation is becoming increasingly important for collecting high-quality demonstrations to teach robots dexterous manipulation skills. For dexterous manipulation, bare-hand tracking provides a practical way to control robotic hands and demonstrate coordinated finger movements without gloves or exoskeletons. However, this type of teleoperation faces two key feedback limitations: a lack of force feedback and visual feedback of occluded region. The absence of force feedback hinders precise and safe manipulation, as operators must infer contact force visually rather than feel them directly. Occlusion by objects or other robot parts impairs the assessment of hand positions, approach distances, and pre-grasp configurations suited to the object's shape. To address these limitations, we propose a digital-twin-based augmented reality (AR) teleoperation interface that integrates bare-hand tracking, bimanual robotic hands, and virtual representations of the robot and task objects. The interface renders tactile measurements on robot hand meshes as visuo-force feedback. Furthermore, it offers a user-controlled virtual view panel or occlusion-aware transparency rendering to provide occlusion-mitigating visual feedback. We evaluated the interface through user studies involving Task 1 and Task 2, examining how force visualization and visual assistance support demonstration collection in tasks requiring careful force regulation and manipulation under occlusion.

</details>


### [56] [PB-STDG: A Prediction-Based Short-Term Decentralized Greedy Guidance Algorithm for a Drone Road System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05122)
*Zhouyu Qu,Andreas Willig,Xiaobing Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PB-STDG算法，通过在信标包中增加信息并引入预测机制，使无人机能预判邻近无人机的决策，从而缓解原STDG算法的过度保守问题，在结构化无人机道路系统中提升交通效率并保持安全水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市环境中无人机应用日益增多，不同主体运营的无人机需协同避碰并确定无冲突的短期飞行路径；原STDG算法存在过度保守的行为，导致交通效率不高。

Method: 提出基于预测的短期分散贪婪（PB-STDG）制导算法，面向结构化无人机道路系统（DRS）。该算法在STDG基础上扩展，通过信标包中共享的额外信息引入预测机制，使无人机能够预判邻近无人机的决策。

Result: 在多个仿真场景中与STDG进行对比评估，结果显示PB-STDG在与STDG相当的安全水平下提升了交通效率。

Conclusion: PB-STDG通过预测机制有效缓解了STDG的过度保守问题，在保证安全性的同时改善了城市空域无人机的交通效率。

Abstract: In recent years, Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) or drones have been increasingly adopted in urban environments for applications such as parcel delivery, infrastructure inspection, emergency response, and drone light shows. A non-negligible issue is how to manage the increasing number of drones operated by different entities, to enable them to cooperatively avoid potential collisions and determine conflict-free short-term flight paths in urban airspace. This paper presents a Prediction-Based Short-Term Decentralized Greedy (PB-STDG) guidance algorithm for a structured Drone Road System (DRS). PB-STDG extends the original STDG algorithm by introducing a prediction mechanism that enables drones to anticipate the decisions of neighboring drones using additional information shared in beacon packets, aiming to address the over-conservative behavior observed in the STDG algorithm. Several simulation scenarios are conducted to evaluate and compare the proposed algorithm with STDG. The results show that PB-STDG improves traffic efficiency while maintaining a safety level comparable to that of STDG.

</details>


### [57] [Recursive Self-Improvement of Visuomotor Policies through Local Recovery Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05151)
*Yuzhi Zhang,Xinyu Liu,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"局部恢复监督"框架，通过多轮审计-纠错示范-策略更新实现视觉运动策略的递归自我改进，仅部署学生策略，在LIBERO-Goal与robomimic Can上均以等量优化步数超越原始数据续训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动策略能完成熟悉任务，但缺乏在自身犯错后进行纠正的行为能力。现有后训练多依赖原始数据续训，难以针对性弥补失败状态上的纠错能力。

Method: 递归自我改进框架：每轮先用离线审计器结合粗粒度与细粒度时序证据定位未解决的失败，并给出可观测的修复目标；由固定的多模态智能体作为"工具使用型教师"，经观察—计算—执行—反馈生成恢复动作；冻结的学生策略检验教师终点能否继续推进，若延续失败则回滚该终点并延长示范；再用动作级质量评估划分连续训练窗口，配合对齐观测、质量权重与有效性掩码进行训练。仅学生策略部署。

Result: LIBERO-Goal初步研究中，恢复增强后训练在同一π0检查点下达成88/100成功回合，原始数据续训为78/100；robomimic Can上BC-RNN用26段局部恢复片段将成功率从102/130提升至112/130。两组对比均匹配2,000额外优化步数。

Conclusion: 以失败状态为中心的局部恢复监督与递归自我改进，可在相同训练预算下有效提升纠错行为与任务成功率，且无需部署教师模型。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies can execute familiar tasks yet lack the corrective behavior needed after their own mistakes. We present a framework for recursive self-improvement through local recovery supervision. Each round audits the current policy, generates corrective demonstrations at supported failure states, and uses them to update the policy that drives the next round of collection. An offline auditor locates unresolved failures using coarse and dense temporal evidence and specifies observable repair goals. A fixed multimodal agent acts as a tool-using teacher, generating recovery actions through observation, computation, execution, and feedback. The frozen student tests whether each teacher endpoint supports further progress. If continuation fails, the system restores that endpoint and extends the demonstration. Action-level quality assessment then defines continuous training windows with aligned observations, quality weights, and validity masks. Only the student is deployed. In a preliminary LIBERO-Goal study, recovery-augmented post-training achieves 88 successful episodes out of 100 validation scenes, compared with 78 for original-data continuation from the same $π_0$ checkpoint. An earlier BC-RNN study on robomimic Can improves success from 102/130 to 112/130 using 26 local recovery segments. Both comparisons match 2,000 additional optimization steps.

</details>


### [58] [VICON: Visual-Inertial-Contact based Hand-Object Tracking for Manipulation Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05180)
*Yubin Jeon,Uiseong Shin,Hwanchul La,Jaeseong Kang,Hyelim Choi,Yongseok Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VICON框架，融合视觉-惯性手套与RGB-D相机，在严重遮挡下同步捕获手物运动、接触点与法向力，构建公开数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作学习依赖能捕捉多样自然手物交互的人类演示数据，其中接触点与接触力提供"在何处、以多大力"交互的监督信号，仅靠运动轨迹无法完整获取。然而，现有方法难以联合捕获手物运动、接触点与力，且手物交互造成的严重遮挡使手与物体的精确跟踪存在挑战。

Method: 1) 采用视觉-惯性手套与RGB-D相机实现精确手部跟踪，重新设计手套以集成接触传感：依据人类抓取频率在手套上布置力敏电阻（FSR），同步记录接触状态与标定后的法向力；2) 无需预先存在的CAD模型，利用RGB-D图像与单目视频重建的网格估计物体位姿；3) 提出基于因子图的物体轨迹估计，融合按可见度加权的物体位姿估计（考虑手物遮挡）、FSR测量与手部运动先验。

Result: 在5个物体、40次动作捕捉会话中，VICON失败帧率仅2.5%，而基线方法为50.9%–64.6%；遮挡下中位误差为3.9 mm与3.0度。基于VICON构建了包含同步手物运动、接触点与法向力的数据集，并将公开释出覆盖10类物体的扩展数据集。

Conclusion: VICON可在严重遮挡下手物交互场景中实现鲁棒的手物跟踪与接触信息采集，显著优于现有基线，并为灵巧操作学习提供高质量同步手物运动-接触力数据集。

Abstract: Learning dexterous manipulation benefits from human demonstration datasets that capture diverse and natural hand-object interactions. In particular, contact points and forces provide supervision on where and how strongly to interact, which cannot be fully captured by motion trajectories alone. However, methods for jointly capturing hand and object motion, contact points, and forces remain limited. Moreover, severe occlusion from hand-object interaction challenges accurate tracking of both hands and objects. To address these limitations, we present a Visual-Inertial-CONtact based hand-object tracking (VICON) framework. It holistically captures both hand and object motion along with contact information during manipulation, even under severe occlusion. First, we adopt a visual-inertial glove and an RGB-D camera for accurate hand tracking, and redesign the glove to incorporate contact sensing. Specifically, force-sensitive resistors (FSRs) are placed on the glove based on human grasp frequency to synchronously record contact states and calibrated normal forces. Second, without requiring pre-existing CAD models, we estimate object poses using RGB-D images and a mesh reconstructed from a monocular video. We propose factor-graph-based object trajectory estimation that fuses object-pose estimates weighted by visibility under hand-object occlusion, FSR measurements, and a hand-motion prior. Across 40 motion-capture sessions with five objects, VICON achieves a 2.5% failed-frame rate compared with 50.9-64.6% for the baselines, with median errors of 3.9 mm and 3.0 degrees under occlusion. Using VICON, we construct a dataset containing synchronized hand-object motion, contact points, and normal forces, and will publicly release an expanded dataset covering 10 object categories at https://github.com/VICON-dataset/dataset.

</details>


### [59] [Vela: Scaling Vision-Language-Action Models with Adaptive Action Curve Parametrization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05230)
*Yifan Li,Jiaxu Wang,Dongming Wu,Yicheng Jiang,Ryan Ji,Xiangyu Yue,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Vela将未来机器人行为表示为连续轨迹，用样条动作表示与运动相关时间支撑提升时间分辨率与长时程覆盖的平衡，在仿真与真机上取得良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型将未来运动表示为固定速率动作块，时间分辨率与预测时域被固定输出预算绑定；逐点表示在高度相关的相邻动作上浪费容量，时序连续性与平滑性需隐式学习，且长时程覆盖与接触密集操作所需的局部精度之间存在取舍。

Method: 提出Vela视觉-语言-动作基础模型：以连续轨迹表示未来机器人行为；结合紧凑的样条动作表示、运动相关的时间支撑（motion-dependent temporal support），以及面向异构本体的共享动作接口，使固定输出预算能随运动自适应调整时间分辨率；在大规模多本体机器人数据上预训练。

Result: 在LIBERO-X、EBench以及两个真实世界长时程任务（蛋糕烘焙与土豆切丝）上评估，在仿真与物理操作中均取得有前景的结果。

Conclusion: 结果凸显连续动作表示作为未来具身基础模型基础的潜力。项目页：https://clementine24.github.io/Vela/ 。

Abstract: Most vision-language-action models represent future motion as fixed-rate action chunks, tying temporal resolution and prediction horizon to a fixed output budget. This pointwise representation wastes capacity on highly correlated neighboring actions, leaves temporal continuity and smoothness to be learned implicitly, and forces a tradeoff between long-horizon coverage and the local precision required for contact-rich manipulation. To address these limitations, we introduce Vela, a vision-language-action foundation model that represents future robot behavior as continuous trajectories. Vela combines a compact spline-based action representation with motion-dependent temporal support and a shared action interface for heterogeneous embodiments, allowing a fixed output budget to adapt its temporal resolution across motions. We pretrain Vela on large-scale multi-embodiment robot data and evaluate it on LIBERO-X, EBench, and two real-world long-horizon tasks, egg-cake cooking and potato shredding, obtaining promising results across simulation and physical manipulation. These results highlight the potential of continuous action representations as a foundation for future embodied foundation models. Project page and more results: https://clementine24.github.io/Vela/ .

</details>


### [60] [Safe Ergodic Control for Multi-Robot Systems via Quadratic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05258)
*Yo Toyomoto,Mohamed Elobaid,Bryce L. Ferguson,Alberto Quattrini Li,Shinkyu Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于二次规划（QP）的控制器，将遍历性目标与安全性约束联合求解，采用时间可微的高斯核遍历度量构造时变控制屏障函数（CBF），实现实时安全遍历控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全遍历控制器依赖离线轨迹优化或分层架构，限制了实时性，且使遍历性目标与安全约束解耦，难以兼顾。作者希望在同一优化框架内同时处理遍历性与安全性。

Method: 提出高斯核遍历度量替代经典基于指示函数的度量，使其对时间可微；将该度量的指数衰减作为时变CBF约束，并引入松弛变量，同时加入区域包含与机器人间避碰的硬CBF约束，统一构建为一个QP控制器。

Result: 理论上证明了所得QP在任意安全初始构型下保持可行，且除去松弛项外遍历度量按指数衰减；仿真显示优于基线方法，三架飞行器的实验验证了方法在真实硬件上的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作通过时间可微遍历度量与CBF的联合QP建模，实现实时、安全且保持遍历性的控制，并提供可行性保证，仿真与实机实验均验证其性能。

Abstract: Ergodic control drives robots to spend time in each region in proportion to a spatial distribution of interest, making it well suited for dense spatiotemporal environmental monitoring. Existing safe ergodic controllers rely on offline trajectory optimization or hierarchical architectures, which limit real-time applicability and decouple the ergodicity objective from the safety constraint. This paper presents a quadratic programming (QP)-based controller that treats ergodicity and safety jointly. We first introduce a Gaussian-kernel ergodic metric that, unlike the classical indicator-based metric, is time differentiable. This allows the exponential decay of the metric to be imposed as a time-varying control barrier function (CBF) constraint, relaxed by a slack variable, alongside hard CBF constraints for region containment and inter-robot collision avoidance. We establish that the resulting QP remains feasible from any safe initial configuration and that, up to the slack term, the ergodic metric decays exponentially. Simulations show improved performance over baseline methods, and experiments with three aerial vehicles validate the work on real hardware.

</details>


### [61] [CoDance: Learning Reactive and Compliant Human-Humanoid Interaction from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05324)
*Zhuoqun Chen,Shucheng Jia,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoDance 提出一个从视频学习人形机器人反应式、柔顺人机交互的框架，以双人舞蹈为挑战性场景，实现人形机器人与人类伙伴持续双手接触的协同运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机合作交互需要人形机器人在与人类持续身体接触下协调运动，既要响应交互力又要保持稳定自然的步态。现有方法难以同时兼顾对伙伴运动的跟随、演示步态风格的保持以及对物理交互力的柔顺响应，因此需要一个能从视频学习此类能力的新框架。

Method: 给定一段两名人类舞者的单目视频，CoDance 将其动作重定向为机器人参考运动与一个移动伙伴。方法引入多连杆柔顺增强（multi-link compliance augmentation），在双手处施加结构化外力，将运动学演示转化为具备力感知的训练数据。基于该数据训练的策略在跟随伙伴的同时保持演示的步态风格，并对物理交互力做出柔顺响应。

Result: 在仿真中，策略能够根据伙伴的变化调整脚步，并复现增强演示中所编码的约 80% 腕部位移。在物理人形机器人上，CoDance 实现了与人类伙伴持续的双人舞蹈，包括前向与后向运动之间的反复切换。

Conclusion: CoDance 通过多连杆柔顺增强从视频中学习反应式柔顺的人机交互策略，在仿真和真实人形机器人上验证了持续双手接触双人舞蹈的可行性，为从视频学习物理人机协作提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Partnered human-humanoid interaction couples locomotion with continuous physical contact. A humanoid needs to coordinate with a person's motion while responding to interaction forces and maintaining stable and natural movement. We present CoDance, a framework for learning reactive and compliant human-humanoid interaction from video. We study partnered dancing as a challenging instantiation, where a humanoid coordinates its footsteps with a moving partner and maintains continuous two-hand contact. Given a single video of two human dancers, CoDance retargets their motions into a robot reference and a moving partner. We introduce a multi-link compliance augmentation that transforms the kinematic demonstration into force-aware training data by adapting the robot reference under structured forces at both hands. Policies trained on this data follow the observed partner while preserving the demonstrated locomotion style and responding compliantly to physical interaction. In simulation, the policies adapt their footsteps to changes in the partner and reproduce approximately 80% of the wrist displacement encoded by the augmented demonstrations. On a physical humanoid, CoDance enables sustained two-hand dancing with a human partner including repeated transitions between forward and backward motions.

</details>


### [62] [VAMPS: Visual and Motor Policies from Sampling-Based Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05331)
*Mohamed Yassine Kabouri,Pietro Noah Crestaz,Quang-Nam Nguyen,Qilong Cheng,Ludovic Righetti,Nicolas Mansard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VAMPS利用MPPI控制生成数据以训练可复用策略，无需人类演示，支持仿真迭代训练本体感知策略并迁移到真机，也支持直接在真实机器人数据上训练视觉运动策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接在物理系统上学习机器人策略面临数据采集成本高与探索不安全的问题，且通常依赖人类演示。作者希望在不依赖人类演示的情况下，安全、低成本地获得可复用策略。

Method: 提出VAMPS框架，以MPPI控制为核心生成训练数据，含两种模式：(1)单步本体感知策略在仿真中迭代训练，策略为MPPI提供热启动，精炼轨迹产生新监督，学习终端价值改善短时域规划，并用IQL评论家指导策略更新；(2)视觉运动策略直接用真机数据，MPPI基于任务特定状态估计规划执行并记录RGB与传感器观测，采用Action Chunking with Transformers离线训练预测动作块以缩短有效预测时域。

Result: 迭代精炼优于在冻结MPPI数据上单次训练；学到的运动策略成功迁移到Unitree Go2。在Flexiv Rizon 10S上实现了视觉运动抓取放置与力感知白板擦除，后者策略额外观测6维力/力矩与期望法向力。

Conclusion: VAMPS证明可在仿真中学习策略再迁移到硬件，也可直接从自主采集的真机数据学习策略，为无人类演示的机器人策略学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Learning robot policies directly on physical systems remains difficult because data collection is costly and policy exploration can be unsafe. We introduce Visual and Motor Policies from Sampling-Based Planning (VAMPS), a framework that uses Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control to train reusable policies without human demonstrations. VAMPS supports two training modes. For one-step proprioceptive policies, it operates iteratively in simulation: the policy warm-starts MPPI, and the refined trajectories provide new supervision as the policy changes. A learned terminal value improves short-horizon planning, while an Implicit Q-Learning (IQL) critic guides the policy update. Iterative refinement outperforms training once on frozen MPPI data, and we transfer the learned locomotion policy to a Unitree Go2. For visuomotor policies, VAMPS operates directly from real-robot data. MPPI uses task-specific state estimates to plan and execute trajectories while recording RGB and sensor observations on a Flexiv Rizon 10S. Action Chunking with Transformers predicts action chunks, reducing the effective prediction horizon, and is trained offline on this fixed dataset. We demonstrate visuomotor pick-and-place and force-aware whiteboard erasing. In the latter task, the policy additionally observes the measured $6$-D wrench and desired normal force. These results show that VAMPS can learn policies either in simulation followed by hardware transfer or directly from autonomously collected real-robot data.

</details>


### [63] [Optimal Control with Learned Critics under Unmodeled State Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05359)
*Philipp Schoch,Markus Ryll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种学习型MPC框架：用残差动力学网络补偿解析模型缺失的状态依赖效应，并用学习到的动作价值critic为局部iLQR注入长时域MDP结构；配合GPU加速的批量iLQR求解器，实现可扩展的基于模型的强化学习，在建模不完整/有偏的控制任务上提升了闭环性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPC具有结构化、约束感知的决策能力，但依赖适合优化的解析动力学模型，难以应对接触等难以建模的状态依赖；而无模型强化学习虽避免建模假设，却需要大量交互数据。作者希望结合两者的优势：既保留局部基于模型规划的数据效率与结构，又用学习组件弥补动力学不完整与有限时域短视问题。

Method: 1) 在标称解析模型上增加残差动力学网络，从数据中学习缺失的状态依赖效应；2) 将所得规划器与学习到的动作价值critic结合，把长时域MDP结构注入局部iLQR优化，缓解有限时域短视；3) 开发GPU加速的批量iLQR求解器，在最优控制循环内评估学习到的动力学与critic网络，并行求解数千个轨迹优化问题；4) 将整套系统集成到机器人仿真器中，实现动力学不完整下可扩展的基于模型强化学习。

Result: 在存在偏差和建模不完整的控制任务上，该方法提升了闭环控制性能，同时保持了基于模型结构，从而支持高效的约束轨迹优化。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将残差动力学学习与长时域价值critic嵌入到可GPU批处理的iLQR最优控制中，能够在保持模型-based规划结构与约束优化效率的同时，显著改善不完整动力学条件下的控制表现，为可扩展的基于模型强化学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Model Predictive Control (MPC) provides a structured and constraint-aware mechanism for decision-making, but its reliance on optimization-friendly analytical dynamics models limits its use in tasks with contacts and other hard-to-model state dependencies. Model-free reinforcement learning avoids explicit modeling assumptions but typically requires large amounts of interaction data. We present a learning-based MPC framework that combines the data efficiency and structure of local model-based planning with learned components that compensate for incomplete dynamics and finite-horizon myopia. The method augments a nominal analytical model with a residual dynamics network that learns missing state-dependent effects from data and combines the resulting planner with a learned action-value critic that injects long-horizon MDP structure into the local iLQR optimization. To make this practical at reinforcement-learning scale, we develop a GPU-accelerated batched iLQR solver that evaluates learned dynamics and critic networks inside the optimal-control loop and solves thousands of trajectory-optimization problems in parallel. The complete system is integrated into a robotics simulator, enabling scalable model-based reinforcement learning under incomplete dynamics. Experiments on biased and incompletely modeled control tasks show that the approach improves closed-loop control performance while preserving the model-based structure needed for efficient constrained trajectory optimization.

</details>


### [64] [TUCO: Curating Simulation Demonstrations for Sim-to-Real Robot Policy Co-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05407)
*Ning Zhu,Mengfei Zhao,Yikai Tang,Zhangyujie Sun,Peihao Li,Dongyue Ni,Jindou Jia,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究面向sim-to-real机器人策略协同训练的数据筛选，提出TUCO方法，利用影响函数从闭环目标行为出发统一度量轨迹级效用与集合级覆盖，并通过性能对齐的子集优化器选取互补演示，在RoboMimic与OmniReset上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真演示可补充稀缺的真实世界数据用于机器人策略协同训练，但如何通过数据筛选主动选择这些演示以服务sim-to-real协同训练仍未被充分探索；现有筛选方法也缺乏统一准则，无法从闭环目标行为出发同时衡量轨迹级效用与集合级覆盖。

Method: 提出TUCO（Trajectory-level Utility and set-level Coverage Optimization）。核心是用影响函数追踪每条源演示对目标域评分rollout的影响；关键洞察是这些影响可分解为对目标回报的整体贡献与跨rollout的变异，从而为轨迹效用和集合覆盖提供共同的闭环度量基础。进一步设计性能对齐的子集优化器，将两类度量组合为统一筛选目标，以减少冗余并选出互补演示。

Result: 在RoboMimic和OmniReset上的大量实验验证了主动仿真数据筛选对sim-to-real策略协同训练的价值，并表明TUCO在单仿真器、sim-to-sim及sim-to-real三种设置下均取得SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 主动的仿真数据筛选对sim-to-real协同训练具有明确价值，TUCO通过闭环统一度量与统一优化目标有效筛选互补演示，显著提升协同训练性能。

Abstract: Simulation demonstrations can supplement scarce real-world data for robot policy co-training. However, the value of using data curation to actively select these demonstrations for sim-to-real co-training remains underexplored. Existing curation methods also lack a unified criterion for measuring trajectory-level utility and set-level coverage from closed-loop target behavior. To address these gaps, we present the first systematic study of data curation for sim-to-real robot policy co-training and propose Trajectory-level Utility and set-level Coverage Optimization (TUCO). TUCO uses influence functions to trace how each source demonstration affects target-domain scoring rollouts. Our key insight is that these effects can be decomposed into an overall contribution to target return and variation across rollouts, providing a common closed-loop basis for measuring trajectory utility and set coverage. We further propose a performance-aligned subset optimizer that combines these measures in a unified curation objective to reduce redundancy and select complementary demonstrations. Extensive experiments on RoboMimic and OmniReset establish the value of active simulation data curation for sim-to-real policy co-training and show that TUCO achieves state-of-the-art performance across single-simulator, sim-to-sim, and sim-to-real settings.

</details>


### [65] [RMMBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Robotic Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05414)
*Huapeng Li,Fuxiang Feng,Jinqiu Fan,Shuo Yang,Fengjiao Chen,Xuezhi Cao,Ran Song,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RMMBench，一个面向视觉语言模型（VLMs）的机器人导航与操作综合评估基准，将高低层具身任务统一到同一框架，构建包含70个规范任务场景的"导航-操作"任务套件，覆盖从局部操作到长时序复合导航的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准缺乏对多样化机器人任务的综合评估方法，评估指标较为受限，难以全面且细粒度地评估VLMs的具身能力。

Method: 构建RMMBench基准：要求机器人在连续空间中理解语言指令并执行长时序任务；将高层与低层具身任务无缝整合到统一框架中，构造包含70个规范任务场景的"导航-操作"任务套件，涵盖从局部操作到长时序复合导航的任务谱系。

Result: 实验结果表明，当前领先的VLMs在执行移动操作任务时，在空间定位方面仍面临重大挑战；同时凸显了在长时序交互中增强机器人空间感知能力的必要性。

Conclusion: RMMBench为VLMs在机器人导航与操作中的评估提供了统一、细粒度的基准，揭示了现有模型在空间定位与长时序交互中的不足，指出提升空间感知能力是后续研究的关键方向。

Abstract: Although the advancement of vision-language models (VLMs) has endowed robots with enhanced environmental understanding and task reasoning, a comprehensive evaluation methodology is important to advance the integration of VLMs in robotic navigation and manipulation. However, current benchmarks lack a comprehensive method to evaluate diverse robotic tasks, and evaluation metrics remain relatively constrained, making it difficult to assess the embodied capabilities of VLMs in a thorough and fine-grained manner. To address this issue, we propose RMMBench, an evaluation benchmark that requires robots to understand language instructions and perform long-horizon tasks in continuous spaces. RMMBench seamlessly integrates high- and low-level embodied tasks into a unified framework, constructing a "navigation-manipulation" task suite comprising 70 canonical task scenarios that range from localized manipulation to long-horizon composite navigation. The results reveal that leading VLMs still face major challenges in spatial localization when performing mobile manipulation tasks, and also highlight the necessity of enhancing the spatial perception capability of robots during long-horizon interactions. RMMBench can be accessed at https://mxxq-stack.github.io/rmmbench-project/

</details>


### [66] [EvoMem-VLA: State-Evolution Memory for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05418)
*Yuheng Na,Zhide Zhong,Junjie He,Junfeng Li,Haodong Yan,Jiaan Wang,Jiaguan Zhu,Yangyang Zheng,Tianyu Huang,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 EvoMem-VLA，通过显式编码历史状态间的变化构建“状态演化记忆”，提升 VLA 模型在长时序、依赖记忆任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型依赖当前观测，证据一旦离开视野即丢失，难以应对长时序、依赖记忆的任务。已有方法采用压缩历史特征或稀疏关键帧，但孤立快照无法让策略判断过去交互中发生了什么变化、下一步该执行什么动作。

Method: 构建状态演化记忆：显式编码并保留历史状态间的变化表征，以保留交互结果证据。具体引入条件增量分词（conditional delta tokenization），将有序帧对编码为方向性、源条件化的增量 token，并关联对应状态证据。共享 VLM 主干支持任务自适应路由：常规长时序任务走直接动作路径，多阶段任务走子任务路径，生成可执行子任务作为动作生成的额外输入。

Result: 每个仿真基准使用单一联合训练策略，成功率：RMBench 80.7%，RoboMME 82.0%，跨两种机器人本体的四个真实任务 83.8%，三项评测均显著超越此前最优。

Conclusion: 通过状态演化记忆与增量 token 编码保留交互变化证据，并结合任务自适应路由，EvoMem-VLA 有效提升长时序、记忆依赖任务的策略性能，具有跨仿真与真实环境的泛化能力。

Abstract: Most vision-language-action (VLA) models rely on current observations and lose task-relevant evidence once it leaves view, limiting performance on long-horizon, memory-dependent tasks. Existing efforts incorporate compressed historical features or sparse visual keyframes. However, isolated snapshots can leave the policy uncertain about what changed during past interactions and which action should follow. To overcome this limitation, we propose EvoMem-VLA, which constructs state-evolution memory by explicitly encoding and retaining observed changes between historical states. These change representations preserve evidence of interaction outcomes, allowing the policy to track task progress beyond isolated snapshots. Specifically, we introduce conditional delta tokenization to encode ordered frame pairs into directional, source-conditioned delta tokens, each associated with its corresponding state evidence. A shared VLM backbone supports task-adaptive routing: normal long-horizon tasks follow a direct action route, whereas multi-stage tasks use a subtask route that generates an executable subtask as an additional input for action generation. With a single jointly trained policy for each simulation benchmark, EvoMem-VLA achieves success rates of 80.7\% on RMBench, 82.0\% on RoboMME and 83.8\% across four real-world tasks spanning two robot embodiments. These results represent substantial improvements over the previous state of the art in all three evaluation settings.

</details>


### [67] [Social Navigation for Tour-guide Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05455)
*Vikram Shree,Jose Nino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于力模型的导览机器人社交导航方法，并设计实验评估其对跟随性、安全感和智能感的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导览机器人在引导用户时需要具备社交性，而现有导航多关注路径最优，忽略了障碍、用户位置与朝向等社会因素对用户体验的影响。

Method: 构建基于力的社交导航模型，纳入障碍、用户位置和朝向等多种社会力；设计实验：用户在已知地图中跟随自主机器人前往目的地，中途完成若干分散注意力的子任务；每位参与者进行多轮实验，每轮采用不同力配置，并以最短路径A*搜索作为基线。通过每轮的主观指标评估。

Result: 通过每轮主观指标收集数据，用于分析力模型对跟随性、感知安全感和感知智能等构念的影响（摘要未给出具体数值结果）。

Conclusion: 每种力均有助于提升机器人对用户的社交性；该力模型及其实验设计可用于系统评估社交导航在用户体验维度上的效果。

Abstract: We propose a force-based model for social navigation of a tour-guide robot. Social forces due to various factors like obstacles, user position and heading, have been accounted for in the model. We claim that each one of these forces makes the robot more sociable to the user and we design an experimental setup for evaluation. In the experiment, the user follows an autonomous robot to a destination in a known map, while undertaking a few simple sub-tasks in the middle, which serve as distractions. For each participant, we run several rounds of the experiment, each with different forces and a shortest path, A*-search baseline model. Using per-round subjective indicators, we propose to study the effect of our force model on constructs such as: follow-ability, perceived safety, and perceived intelligence.

</details>


### [68] [FLEX-WAM: Flexible Block-Causal World-Action Models for Long-Horizon Imagination and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05483)
*R. Khorrambakht,Joseph Amigo,Félix Lebel,Leon Seetoo,Jean Ponce,Zhenzhen Li,Ludovic Righetti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FLEX-WAM是一个灵活高效的块因果世界-动作模型，支持变长上下文、非因果预测视野和无限自回归生成，通过块因果架构与流匹配梯度平衡实现稳定长时程联合 rollout，并在仿真与真机上验证了规划与策略推断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联合视频-动作模型多采用计算量大的固定视野骨干网络，不适合流式推理和稳定的长时程开环 rollout；且联合训练常产生对指令动作响应薄弱的虚假未来。论文旨在构建统一、高效、可实时部署的世界-动作模型，并解决动作响应性问题。

Method: 提出 FLEX-WAM：采用块因果、可 KV 缓存的架构，结合轴向注意力（axial attention）与分块扩散强制（blockwise diffusion forcing），支持变长上下文、非因果预测视野以及逐帧/逐块无限自回归生成，并可在部署时权衡延迟与吞吐而无需重训。针对动作响应弱的问题，在状态-动作扩散噪声网格上平衡状态与动作流匹配梯度贡献，并使用前向动力学弹性（FD elasticity）作为训练期动作响应性代理来调节世界模型采样。

Result: 在仿真与真实数据集上，FLEX-WAM 取得更优的多步预测质量和延迟，并产生数千步稳定的联合状态-动作 rollout。作为 MCTS 中的联合动作提议器与模拟器，它完全在想象中解决了长时程 PushT 及全部五个 OGBench Puzzle-4x4 任务。在双臂 OpenArm 机器人上，单一 checkpoint 同时充当 play 策略与预期结果预测器，可实时识别并收集模型-现实不匹配以支持未来自我改进。

Conclusion: FLEX-WAM 通过块因果可缓存架构与动作响应性正则，实现了高效、稳定、长时程的统一世界建模与策略推断，验证了其在想象规划与真实机器人自主改进中的实用价值。

Abstract: World--action models (WAMs) promise a unified model that predicts action-conditioned futures, generates feasible actions, and supports planning in imagination. However, existing joint video--action models often use computationally heavy, fixed-horizon backbones ill-suited to streaming inference and stable long-horizon open-loop rollouts. We introduce FLEX-WAM, a Flexible and Efficient Block-Causal World--Action Model for unified simulation and policy inference. FLEX-WAM supports variable-length contexts and non-causal prediction horizons, as well as infinite autoregressive generation frame by frame or block by block. Its block-causal, KV-cacheable architecture combines axial attention and blockwise diffusion forcing to enable efficient real-time rollout and deployment-time latency--throughput tradeoffs without retraining. Joint training can nevertheless produce plausible futures that weakly respond to commanded actions. We address this failure mode by balancing state and action flow-matching gradient contributions across the state--action diffusion-noise grid and regulating world-model sampling using Forward-Dynamics (FD) elasticity, an efficient training-time proxy for action responsiveness. Across simulated and real-world datasets, FLEX-WAM achieves superior multi-step prediction quality and latency while producing stable joint state--action rollouts for thousands of steps. As a joint action proposer and simulator within MCTS, it solves long-horizon PushT and all five OGBench Puzzle-4x4 tasks entirely in imagination. On a bimanual OpenArm-based robot, a single checkpoint jointly serves as a play policy and expected-outcome predictor, enabling real-time identification and collection of model--reality mismatches for future self-improvement.

</details>


### [69] [Robust Surgical Robotic Instrument Tracking via Sequential Multi-Cue Fusion and Sim-to-Real Self-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05491)
*Hanyang Hu,Zekai Liang,Florian Richter,Michael C. Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出跟踪器引导的自训练框架，将合成图像预训练的关键点检测器适配到无标注真实手术视频，通过不确定性感知EKF与RTS平滑生成伪标签，实现精度与速度双优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人器械跟踪对机器人辅助微创手术至关重要，但手术场景复杂、器械几何形状特殊，导致跟踪困难。基于关键点的方法虽高效，却依赖可靠的特征检测，而真实场景下的精确标注成本高昂、难以规模化获取，限制了检测器性能提升。

Method: 提出跟踪器引导的自训练框架：先以合成图像预训练模型，再适配到无标注真实视频。利用已知机器人关节状态，通过不确定性感知EKF递归校正器械位姿与可观测关节角，依据投影模型特征与检测到的关键点、轴边界及掩膜线索进行比对；随后用RTS平滑器细化轨迹，并投影生成伪标签，微调特征检测器，无需繁琐位姿标注。

Result: 在真实视频实验中，自训练在所有评估的关键点指标上均带来一致提升；所得模型在精度和运行时间两方面均优于既有方法。

Conclusion: 跟踪器引导的自训练可有效利用无标注真实视频，缓解真实标注稀缺问题，提升手术器械关键点检测与跟踪性能；代码与数据将在发表后公开。

Abstract: Efficient and robust tracking of surgical robotic instruments is important for robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery, yet remains challenging due to the complexity of surgical scenes and the unconventional geometry of surgical instruments. Keypoint-based approaches are efficient, but their performance depends on reliable feature detection. Improving these detectors with real-world supervision is difficult because accurate real-world annotations are costly to obtain at scale. To address this limitation, we introduce a tracker-guided self-training framework that adapts a model pretrained on synthetic images to unlabeled real-world videos. Given measured robot joint states, an uncertainty-aware EKF recursively corrects the instrument pose and the observable joint angles by comparing projected model features with detected keypoints, shaft boundaries, and mask-derived cues. An RTS smoother subsequently refines the resulting trajectory, which is projected into pseudo-labels for fine-tuning the feature detector without laborious pose annotations. Experiments on real-world videos demonstrate consistent improvements from self-training across all evaluated keypoint metrics, and the resulting model outperforms prior approaches in both accuracy and runtime. The code and data will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [70] [Robust 2D Traversability Mapping for Construction AMRs via Failure-Mode-Aware Fusion of LiDAR Geometry and Monocular Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05505)
*Manoj Karnekar,Om Mandhane,Gautham Ramkumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向活跃建筑工地的实时、失效模式感知的多模态可通行性管线：以LiDAR为几何安全主估计，单目语义作为类别与置信度门控的选择性修正信号，在Jetson AGX Orin上运行，并发布配套多模态数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMR在活跃建筑工地导航面临两类互补失效：几何可通行性建图（如LiDAR）会漏检视觉危险但几何平坦的表面（湿泥、积水混凝土），同时在可行驶减速带与坡道等突变几何处产生"幽灵障碍"。单一模态难以同时解决这两类问题。

Method: 在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上构建实时管线：LiDAR作为几何安全主估计，单目语义作为选择性、类别与置信度门控的修正信号。表示中保留细粒度可通行类别（平坦道路、地形、岩石地形）并标记施工危险。通过将LiDAR投影到稠密语义掩膜、用形态学修复解决稀疏性，并结合Patchwork++进行失效模式感知融合，修正互补的几何失效模式，输出局部AMR代价地图。

Result: 发布来自定制AMR的多模态建筑工地数据集：两个活跃工地的四条闭环ROS 2序列（RGB、深度、LiDAR、IMU、GPS-RTK、里程计），以及覆盖28个语义类别的506帧标注。管线可修正互补的几何失效模式并生成局部代价地图。

Conclusion: 通过失效模式感知的多模态融合，以几何为主、语义门控修正为辅，可同时缓解漏检平坦危险表面与误报突变几何两类问题，为建筑工地AMR提供可通行性建图方案及公开数据集支持。

Abstract: Autonomous Mobile Robots (AMRs) on active construction sites face severe navigational challenges: geometry-based traversability mapping (e.g., LiDAR) misses visually hazardous but geometrically flat surfaces like wet mud and ponding concrete, while abrupt geometry on drivable speed-breakers and inclines produces phantom obstacles. We propose a real-time, failure-mode-aware multimodal traversability pipeline on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin, where LiDAR is the primary geometric safety estimate and monocular semantics act as a selective, class- and confidence-gated corrective signal. The representation retains distinct traversable classes, namely flat road, terrain, and rocky terrain, while flagging construction hazards. We also release a multimodal construction-site dataset from a custom AMR: four closed-loop ROS 2 sequences from two active sites (RGB, depth, LiDAR, IMU, GPS-RTK, odometry) plus 506 annotated frames across 28 semantic classes. By projecting LiDAR onto dense semantic masks, resolving sparsity via morphological in-painting, and applying failure-mode-aware fusion with Patchwork++, the system corrects complementary geometric failure modes for a local AMR costmap.

</details>


### [71] [Neural Barriers: An Online Certifiable Learning-enhanced Adaptive High Order Safety Critical Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05542)
*Lishuo Pan,Mattia Catellani,An Cao,Lorenzo Sabattini,Nora Ayanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可认证的在线学习增强鲁棒自适应控制障碍函数方法，利用Neural ODE适应扰动并结合Conformal Prediction量化适应不确定性，在未知时变模型扰动下提供概率安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制障碍函数能形式化认证系统安全性，但迁移到真实机器人系统需要高模型保真度。实际中载荷变化或风扰等会造成显著模型扰动，导致安全性受损。

Method: 提出Certifiable Online Learning-Enhanced Robust Adaptive Control Barrier Function：使用Neural ODE进行扰动自适应，用Conformal Prediction量化适应不确定性；不确定性高时采用保守策略，随数据增多自适应降低保守性；在模型与轨迹的Lipschitz光滑性假设下给出概率安全界证明。

Result: 在未知时变模型扰动下实现全时域安全保障；通过保守策略应对高不确定性，并随数据积累有效降低控制器保守性；提供了带有概率界的安全保证。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了作为实用安全控制器应用于存在模型扰动的机器人系统的潜力。

Abstract: Control barrier functions are an effective model-based tool to formally certify the safety of a system. However, transferring their theoretical guarantees to real-world robotics systems requires high model fidelity. For example, payloads or wind disturbances can cause significant model perturbations to an aerial vehicle, leading to safety compromises. In this work, we propose a certifiable online learning-enhanced robust adaptive control barrier function, which adapts to disturbances using a Neural ODE and quantifies its adaptation uncertainty with conformal prediction. Our approach guarantees safety at all time under unknown time-varying model disturbances. It adopts a conservative strategy when the adaptation uncertainty is high; and efficiently adapts to reduce controller conservativeness as it receives more data. Our approach provides a provable safety guarantee with a probability bound under suitable Lipschitz smoothness assumptions on the underlying model and trajectory. These results demonstrate the potential of our method as a practical safety controller for robotics system operating under model perturbations.

</details>


### [72] [GR-LIO: A Local Ground-Aware LiDAR-Inertial Odometry System Using Body-to-Ground Height](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05546)
*Zhixin Zhang,Yang Song,Liang Zhao,Nathan Shankar,Barry Lennox,Pawel Ladosz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于滤波器的局部地面感知LIO框架，将局部地面平面几何显式引入状态估计，提升定位精度与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LIO系统对局部地面提供的几何约束利用不足，地面几何信息未被充分挖掘用于状态估计。

Method: 以机器人姿态与体-地(B-G)高度参数化局部地面平面并在滤波器状态中连续传播；基于B-G几何模型实现高效可靠的地面分割，将地面点通过点到面约束引入滤波更新；引入平面运动更新，利用传播的地面平面作为额外约束抑制垂直漂移；设计B-G高度初始化策略并进行在线标定持续优化。

Result: 在多个公开基准数据集及自采真实场景数据集上评估，定位精度与计算效率均持续优于代表性LIO方法。

Conclusion: 显式建模局部地面几何可有效提升地面机器人LIO的估计精度、鲁棒性与效率。

Abstract: LiDAR-inertial odometry (LIO) is widely used for state estimation in ground-based autonomous mobile robots. However, the geometric constraints provided by the local ground surface remain largely underexploited in existing LIO systems. This paper proposes a filter-based local ground-aware LIO framework that explicitly incorporates local ground plane geometry into the state estimation process to improve both localization accuracy and computational efficiency. Specifically, a local ground plane is parameterized by the robot orientation and the body-to-ground (B-G) height and continuously propagated within the state estimation process. Based on the proposed B-G geometry model, a propagated local ground plane enables efficient and reliable ground segmentation. The segmented ground points are then incorporated into the filter update through point-to-plane geometric constraints, improving both state estimation accuracy and efficiency. Furthermore, a planar motion update is introduced to exploit the propagated local ground plane as an additional geometric constraint, effectively suppressing vertical drift and improving estimation robustness.
