Radar2Plan connects sensor encoders, scene representations, and planning heads through common interfaces, enabling controlled comparisons between different sensor and planner configurations. Using DSERT-RoLL and MAN TruckScenes, we evaluated seven combinations of camera, 4D radar, and LiDAR in four representative planning baselines and 12 weather and illumination conditions under a unified protocol. To our knowledge, Radar2Plan is the first benchmark dedicated to evaluating real-world 4D radars for autonomous driving planning. Experiments show that 4D radar alone supports competitive ego-trajectory planning and robust performance under adverse conditions. Sensor-configuration comparisons further demonstrate its complementary value to other modalities, while revealing dependencies on sensor combination and planning architecture. The modular design also supports additional datasets, sensing modalities, and planners, providing a flexible foundation for future research on radar-based autonomous driving planning.

</details>


### [110] [I-BFM: Reward-Conditioned Robust Humanoid Interaction via Unsupervised Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06129)
*Ziqi Han,Yitang Li,Junhan Sun,Fanrong Dong,Yaojie Shen,Lei Ye,Zetong Jing,Yongqi Zhang,Yiming Zhang,Xue Wang,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 I-BFM，首个面向人形-物体交互的行为基础模型。它借助前向-后向表示与无监督强化学习，学习人形、物体及其接触耦合动力学的共享表示；给定下游任务奖励，同一策略可直接以隐命令为条件执行闭环交互，无需任务特定策略优化。单一策略支持搬运、推、踢及目标到达、动作跟踪、风格控制与长时程任务链，并在大幅偏离标称执行后仍保持有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为基础模型（BFM）已证明单一类人策略可支持多样全身控制，但将这种通用性扩展到物理交互仍具挑战。现有方法依赖任务特定策略或参考轨迹跟踪，难以泛化至人形-物体交互任务，且在交互失败或外部扰动后鲁棒性差。

Method: 采用前向-后向表示（forward-backward representations）结合无监督强化学习，学习人形、物体及其接触之间耦合动力学的共享表示。下游任务仅需给定任务奖励，同一策略即可直接以隐命令（latent command）为条件执行闭环交互，无需任务特定策略优化。为进一步改善不同时间尺度上的交互控制，策略同时以短时程交互目标与长时程目标进行训练。

Result: 单一 I-BFM 策略可执行搬运、推、踢，并支持目标到达、动作跟踪、风格控制与长时程任务链。在 Carry 任务上达到 94.3% 标称成功率，机器人摔倒后仍保持 89.3% 成功率，而基于规划的基线仅为 1.3%。在 Unitree G1 真机实验中，展示了多样的移动操作行为、从交互失败和外部扰动中快速恢复，以及无需任务特定重训练的任务链。

Conclusion: I-BFM 是首个面向人形-物体交互的行为基础模型，通过共享耦合动力学表示与隐命令条件化，实现单一策略的通用、闭环、跨时间尺度交互控制，并在大幅偏离标称执行后仍保持有效，仿真与真机实验均验证了其通用性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Behavioral foundation models (BFMs) have recently shown that a single humanoid policy can support diverse whole-body control, but extending such generality to physical interaction remains challenging. We introduce I-BFM, to our knowledge the first BFM for humanoid-object interaction. Rather than relying on task-specific policies or reference tracking, I-BFM learns a shared representation of the coupled dynamics among the humanoid, objects, and their contacts using forward-backward representations and unsupervised reinforcement learning. Given a downstream task reward, the same policy can be directly conditioned on a latent command to execute closed-loop interaction without task-specific policy optimization. To improve interaction control over different time scales, we further train the policy with both short-horizon interaction targets and longer-horizon goal targets. A single I-BFM policy performs carrying, pushing, and kicking, while also supporting goal reaching, motion tracking, stylistic control, and long-horizon task chaining. More importantly, it remains effective after large deviations from nominal execution: on Carry, I-BFM achieves 94.3% nominal success and retains 89.3% success after robot falls, compared with 1.3% for a planning-based baseline. Real-world experiments on a Unitree G1 further demonstrate diverse loco-manipulation behaviors, rapid recovery from interaction failures and external disturbances, and task chaining without task-specific retraining.

</details>


### [111] [Robotizing Human Videos with Physically Consistent Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06137)
*Ching-Lam Cheng,Shengfeng He,Bin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过交互几何重建与深度感知合成，将人手视频改写为机器人可用的操作数据，并与机器人演示协同训练，在RoboTwin任务中取得最高平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频提供可扩展的操作数据，但人手与机器人末端执行器之间存在具身差距，难以直接使用。现有视频编辑方法（如Masquerade）用渲染机器人替换人手，但交互重建不准确、合成不一致，导致抓取不连贯以及机器人-物体遮挡不合理。

Method: 提出两个互补的物理模块：(1) 交互感知接触重建模块，结合手-物分割与网格级接触预测恢复稠密3D接触，并将其转换为时间稳定的平行夹爪抓取；(2) 深度感知合成模块，利用场景与机器人深度信息强制实现物理一致的机器人-物体遮挡。生成的视频保留人类演示的交互结构且与机器人兼容，并与机器人演示协同训练。

Result: 在相同人类视频与机器人数据下，与纯机器人训练及原始Masquerade流程对比，方法在四个RoboTwin任务、两种Diffusion Policy视觉编码器上取得最高平均成功率，在分布外场景变化下增益尤为显著。真实世界部署显示协同训练在任务几何可观测时提升对视觉干扰物的鲁棒性，但深度敏感抓取仍受单相机设置限制。

Conclusion: 从交互几何与场景可见性两方面修复视频编辑的物理不一致性，可有效将人类视频转化为机器人可用的操作数据，提升策略在分布外场景下的成功率与鲁棒性；深度敏感抓取的限制指向多视角/深度传感的未来改进方向。

Abstract: Human videos offer scalable manipulation data, but the embodiment gap between human hands and robot manipulators limits their direct use. Existing video-editing methods replace hands with rendered robots, yet inaccurate interaction reconstruction and compositing can produce inconsistent grasps and implausible robot-object occlusions. We address these failures from two complementary physical aspects: interaction geometry and scene visibility. First, an interaction-aware contact reconstruction module combines hand-object segmentation with mesh-level contact prediction to recover dense 3D contacts, then converts them into temporally stabilized grasps for parallel-jaw grippers. Second, a depth-aware compositing module uses scene and robot depth to enforce physically consistent robot-object occlusions. The resulting videos preserve the interaction structure of human demonstrations in a robot-compatible form and are co-trained with robot demonstrations. Using identical human videos and robot data, we compare against robot-only training and the original Masquerade pipeline. Across four RoboTwin tasks and two Diffusion Policy visual encoders, our method achieves the highest average success rates, with especially strong gains under out-of-distribution scene variation. Real-world deployment further shows that the proposed co-training approach improves robustness to visual distractors when the task geometry is observable, while performance on depth-sensitive grasps remains limited by the single-camera setup.

</details>


### [112] [Talk, Render, Act: Integrating Social Gesture and Digital Face with Synchronized Speech for Conversational Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06153)
*Jin Jiang,Kun Li,Jiancong Ma,Shengcai Liao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TRABot框架，通过多智能体协同实现人形机器人语音、面部动画与身体手势的连贯多模态交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全身体人形机器人常缺乏视觉表现力的面部，语音与手势割裂；而说话面部动画与机器人手势生成通常被分开研发，难以形成协调统一的响应，因此需要一体化方案。

Method: TRABot为智能体框架，包含动作原子构建、对话生成、动作规划与面部动画等专门智能体：（1）构建Robot-Ready语义动作原子，将G1重定向后BEAT2长时动作按自然手势边界切分，标注经人工验证的交际功能与可行轨迹；（2）引入语义条件组合规划器，依据有序语义功能序列与估计的响应对长，在考虑过渡与中性恢复下选取已审核原子，规划最长可行动作序列；（3）在实体G1上部署流式面部-语音-身体集成系统，统一实时交互回路。

Result: 定量与定性实验表明，TRABot在自然度、表现力与多模态连贯性方面的总体表现优于所有对比条件。

Conclusion: TRABot验证了以多智能体语义规划与流式集成实现语音-面部-身体协同生成的有效性，为富有表现力的人形机器人交互提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Expressive humanoid interaction requires speech, facial animation, and body gestures to form a coherent response. However, many full-body humanoid robots produce speech and gestures without a visually expressive face, while talking-face animation and robot gesture generation are typically developed separately. We present Talk, Render, Act (TRABot), an agent-based framework comprising specialized agents for motion-atom construction, dialogue generation, motion planning, and facial animation. First, to produce natural and semantically meaningful gestures, we construct Robot-Ready Semantic Motion Atoms by segmenting long-form, G1-retargeted BEAT2 motion into units with natural gesture boundaries, human-verified communicative functions, and feasible trajectories. Second, to preserve semantic order and coordinate body motion with the spoken response, we introduce a Semantic-Conditioned Compositional Planner. Given an ordered semantic function sequence and an estimated response duration, the planner selects approved atoms to realize the longest feasible action sequence while accounting for transitions and neutral recovery. Finally, we deploy a Streaming Face-Speech-Body Integration system on a physical G1 humanoid, combining streaming dialogue audio, audio-driven facial animation, and semantically planned body motion in a unified real-time interaction loop. Quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that TRAbot achieves the best overall performance among all compared conditions in terms of naturalness, expressiveness, and multimodal coherence.

</details>


### [113] [Controllable and Photorealistic Pedestrian Risky Motion Generation for End-to-End Driving Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06171)
*Siyuan Liu,Miao Li,Haibao Yu,Haohong Lin,Qing Zhou,Bingbing Nie,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ControlPed 结合轨迹级冲突合成与 3D 高斯泼溅（3DGS），生成照片级真实、运动可控的安全关键行人场景，用于端到端自动驾驶安全评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估端到端自动驾驶在罕见且安全关键的车辆-行人交互中的表现，需要照片级真实、传感器级别的场景。但现有方法存在两难：基于轨迹的场景生成器无法合成原始视觉观测，而基于视频的方法缺乏可控性。

Method: ControlPed 框架融合轨迹级冲突合成与 3DGS。基于 HazardPed 数据集（10,352 段交通视频、422 条冲突轨迹、高清地图及 857 个标注 3D 人体动作），首先生成冲突轨迹，通过文本条件运动扩散将其提升为 3D 人体运动序列，最后使用可动画 3DGS 化身渲染多视角传感器观测。

Result: 在 88 个渲染出的照片级真实场景中进行安全评估，七个领先的端到端驾驶模型性能严重下降，平均 HDScore 从 88.8 骤降至 47.4，暴露出在危险行人行为下的主要失效模式。

Conclusion: ControlPed 弥合了轨迹级可控性与视频级真实感之间的鸿沟，可生成照片级真实、运动可控的安全关键行人场景，为车辆-行人交互的安全评估提供有效基准。数据集与测试基准将公开以促进相关安全评估研究。

Abstract: Evaluating end-to-end autonomous driving under rare, safety-critical vehicle-pedestrian interactions requires photorealistic, sensor-level scenarios. However, trajectory-based scenario generators cannot synthesize raw visual observations, whereas video-based approaches lack controllability. To bridge this gap, we present ControlPed, a novel framework that combines trajectory-level conflict synthesis with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) to generate photorealistic, motion-controllable safety-critical scenarios. Built upon HazardPed, a dataset derived from 10,352 traffic videos comprising 422 conflict trajectories, HD maps, and 857 annotated 3D human motions, ControlPed first generates conflict trajectories, lifts them into 3D human motion sequences via text-conditioned motion diffusion, and finally renders multi-view sensor observations using animatable 3DGS avatars. Safety evaluation in 88 rendered photorealistic scenarios reveals that seven leading end-to-end driving models suffer a severe performance drop, with their mean HDScore plunging from 88.8 to 47.4, exposing major failure modes under dangerous pedestrian behaviors. The dataset and testing benchmarks will be released to facilitate safety assessment of vehicle-pedestrian interactions.

</details>


### [114] [Arm-wise Compositional Generalization in Dual-Arm Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06184)
*Zaibin Zhang,Binghao Ran,Yuhan Wu,Zhongbo Zhang,Yifan Wang,Junwei Jiang,Junlan Xiao,Wangcheng Shi,Li Kang,Yiran Qin,Zhenfei Yin,Lijun Wang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 ACG-Bench，一个针对双臂策略“臂间组合泛化”的基准，包含 23 个任务-条件对、8 个任务族，其中 17 个为未见组合；并基于统一 VLA 主干比较数据增强与架构策略，提出 AE-VLA，在仿真与实体机器人上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂协作中的泛化可被理解为以新方式组合熟悉的原子技能，但现有评测难以揭示在不同协调需求下哪些训练与架构选择真正支持这种能力，缺乏统一测试平台与可比较结论。

Method: 构建 ACG-Bench 基准：23 个任务-条件对、8 个任务族，含 6 个域内条件与 17 个未见组合（重排序、同步、二者结合、跨任务组合）；所有方法接收相同逐臂原子提示，成功需满足任务目标及物理里程碑与顺序/时序约束。以 π0.5 为统一 VLA 主干，在共享源数据与统一评测协议下比较代表性数据增强与架构策略，架构研究涵盖臂 token 分组、技能特定 LoRA 适配器（SkillLoRA）与臂间注意力（AWA），并组合为 AE-VLA。

Result: 仿真中 AE-VLA 泛化成功率达 21.53%，对比单 π0.5 的 2.94%、MA-VLA 的 3.06%、两个独立控制 π0.5 策略的 5.53%；在实体 SO101 机器人上，AE-VLA 在五个未见条件下平均成功率为 39.00%，最强基线为 10.00%。

Conclusion: 技能条件化参数与注意力结构具有互补性；研究为设计能超越固定训练流程、具备组合泛化能力的双臂策略提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Generalization in multi-arm collaboration can be studied as composing familiar atomic skills in new ways across arms. However, existing evaluations offer limited insight into which training and architectural choices support this ability under different coordination requirements. We introduce \textbf{ACG-Bench}, a benchmark for \emph{Arm-wise Compositional Generalization} that provides a common testbed for studying skill recomposition in dual-arm policies. It contains 23 task--condition pairs across 8 task families, with 6 in-domain conditions and 17 unseen compositions covering reordering, synchronization, their combination, and cross-task composition. All methods receive the same per-arm atomic prompts, and success requires achieving the task goal while satisfying physical milestones and specified order or timing constraints. Using $π_{0.5}$ as a common vision-language-action backbone, we compare representative data-augmentation and architectural strategies with shared source data and a common evaluation protocol. Our architectural study examines arm-token grouping, skill-specific LoRA adapters (SkillLoRA), and arm-wise attention (AWA), highlighting the complementarity of skill-conditioned parameters and attention structure. Combining these choices yields \textbf{AE-VLA}, which achieves 21.53\% generalization success in simulation, compared with 2.94\% for Single $π_{0.5}$, 3.06\% for MA-VLA, and 5.53\% for two independently controlled $π_{0.5}$ policies. On physical SO101 robots, AE-VLA reaches 39.00\% mean success across five unseen conditions, compared with 10.00\% for the strongest baseline. These findings provide empirical guidance for designing dual-arm policies that generalize beyond fixed training routines.

</details>


### [115] [STC-MPM: Coupled Deformation, Progressive Damage, and Cut Formation in Soft-Tissue Cutting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06198)
*Kewei Zuo,Jiahe Chen,Ziao Kuang,Junsheng Zhu,Etsuko Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: STC-MPM：耦合有限应变、损伤演化与失效后处理的软组织切割仿真框架，无需预置切口即可联合分析切口形成与组织响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人自动化中的切割操作（从食品加工到手术组织切除）常面对高顺应性目标：刀刃运动可能使材料变形或位移而非推进预期切口，渐进破坏又改变载荷传递与后续刀具-组织交互。因此计算描述必须把切口形成与不断演化的力学响应联系起来，而不能独立于材料失效预先指定切口。

Method: 提出STC-MPM框架，基于物质点法，耦合三项要素：有限应变变形、历史驱动的连续介质损伤、可配置的失效后处理策略。损伤仅在刀刃过程区内拉伸应变历史超过材料阈值时萌生；渐进退化在应用失效后策略之前即改变应力传递，残余组织继续变形、移动并与刀具交互。数值研究采用延迟粒子失活。控制损伤速率上限进行对照实验。

Result: 在相同刀刃运动下，降低损伤速率上限不改变首次记录到损伤的时间，但会延迟并提高峰值反作用力、延迟粒子失活，并增大最大插入深度处的固定队列位移统计量。该匹配对照表明，萌生之后的失效演化会改变后续刀具载荷与记录到的材料运动。

Conclusion: STC-MPM能够在无预置切割界面的条件下，支持对切口形成及其伴随组织响应的联合分析。

Abstract: Cutting is a recurring operation in robotic au tomation, from food preparation to surgical tissue resection. For highly compliant targets, blade motion may deform or displace the material rather than advance the intended cut, while progressive failure changes load transfer and subsequent tool tissue interaction. A computational description must therefore connect cut formation to the evolving mechanical response, rather than specifying the incision independently of material failure. We present STC-MPM (Soft Tissue Cutting with the Material Point Method), a framework that couples finite-strain deformation, history-driven continuum damage, and configurable post-failure treatment. Damage initiates only when a tensile strain history exceeds a material threshold within the blade process zone. Progressive degradation changes stress transmission before the post-failure policy is applied, while the remaining tissue continues to deform, move, and interact with the tool. Numerical scalpel studies using delayed particle deactivation follow this response through insertion and withdrawal. Under identical blade motion, reducing the damage-rate limit leaves the first recorded damage time unchanged but delays and increases peak reaction force, delays particle deactivation, and increases the fixed-cohort displacement statistic at maximum insertion. The matched comparison illustrates how post-initiation failure evolution alters subsequent tool loading and recorded material motion. STC-MPM thus supports joint analysis of cut formation and accompanying tissue response without a pre-inserted cutting interface.

</details>


### [116] [Towards Quadruped-Provided Localization and Active Tracking for Micro-UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06215)
*Alejandro Lorite Mora,Andrés Faíña*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出将微型无人机的定位计算从机上转移到配备7自由度机械臂的四足机器人上，实现“地面机器人定位空中伙伴”，使仅带IMU的27g微型无人机获得完整6自由度位姿并自主飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型无人机机身小、可进入大型机器人无法到达的狭小空间，但载荷能力不足，无法携带自主飞行所需的感知与计算资源。因此需要一种将定位负担外移的方案，同时解决现有外部相机仅能相对跟踪、无法在共享地图中定位无人机的问题。

Method: 将整个定位栈从空中平台移至四足机器人（配7自由度机械臂）。末端执行器相机检测无人机上的AprilTag基准标记，并与四足机器人自身定位融合，把无人机置于共享地图坐标系中。机械臂作为主动观测器，随两机器人运动重新调整位置以保持无人机在视野内。无人机仅携带IMU，融合外部位姿输入以跟踪指令设定点。

Result: 实验飞行中外部位姿精度达12–16 mm，足以驱动无人机自主飞行，其控制效果与动捕反馈控制在2–5 cm范围内相当。四足机器人主动跟随无人机可将跟踪误差从11.0 cm降至6.9 cm，原因是相机保持在近距离区间，此时标记精度最高。

Conclusion: 将定位栈外移至带臂四足机器人，可为极轻量微型无人机提供高精度共享地图位姿，实现接近动捕水平的自主飞行；主动观测策略通过维持近距离视野显著提升跟踪精度。

Abstract: Micro unmanned aerial vehicles (micro-UAVs) are small enough to reach confined spaces that larger robots cannot access, but too small to carry the sensing and computing power required for autonomous flight. We move the localization stack entirely off the aerial platform onto a quadruped robot with a 7-degree-of-freedom (DOF) arm, which supplies the micro-UAV (27 g bare, 42 g with fiducial markers) its full 6-DOF pose. A camera at the arm's end-effector detects AprilTag fiducial markers on the drone and composes that observation with the quadruped's own self-localization to place the drone in a shared map frame, so the ground robot localizes its partner, rather than only tracking it relative to the camera. The arm acts as an actively-controlled observer, repositioning to keep the drone in view as both robots move; the drone carries only an inertial measurement unit and fuses the external pose to fly commanded setpoints. In lab flights the external pose is accurate to 12-16 mm, enough to fly the drone autonomously within 2-5 cm of motion-capture-fed control. Having the quadruped actively follow the drone reduces the tracking error from 11.0 cm to 6.9 cm by holding the camera in the close range, where the markers are most accurate.

</details>


### [117] [AUTOPILOT An Advanced Perception, Localization and Path Planning Techniques for Autonomous Vehicles Using YOLOv7 and MiDaS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06232)
*Harshkumar Devmurari,Gautham Kuckian,Prajjwal Vishwakarma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文实现了一个集成YOLO目标检测、MiDaS深度感知、透视变换与决策路径规划的自动驾驶感知-定位系统，并通过仿真与实验验证其鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶是能够变革交通与出行的可靠技术，但其发展依赖感知、定位、决策与控制等多领域的显著进步。本文旨在通过整合目标检测与深度感知，为环境中障碍物的感知与定位提供新的鲁棒方案。

Method: 基于项目实现，将YOLO（You Only Look Once）用于目标检测，MiDaS用于深度感知，从而实现障碍物的定位与感知；并结合透视变换与用于路径规划的决策模块，构建组合系统。同时评述了目标检测、深度感知、定位与路径规划的当代技术现状，并通过仿真与实验评估组合系统性能。

Result: 结果表明，YOLO与MiDaS的组合为目标检测与深度感知提供了一个新的鲁棒系统。

Conclusion: 本研究推动了自动驾驶技术的进步，为环境中障碍物的感知与定位提供了新颖方法。

Abstract: Self driving vehicles have emerged as a reliable technology that has the capability to transform transportation and mobility. The development of self driving cars requires significant advances in a number of areas, including perception, localization, decision making, and control. This research paper is based on the project implementation of the combination of object detection using YOLO (You Only Look Once), depth sensing using MiDaS for the localization and perception of obstacles, perspective transform, and decision making for path planning in self driving cars. The contemporary state of the technology for object detection, depth sensing, localization, and path planning evaluates the performance of the combined system through simulations and experiments. The results show that the combination of YOLO and MiDaS provides a new robust system for object detection and depth sensing. This research paper contributes to the advancement of self driving car technology and provides new and innovative approaches to the perception and localization of obstacles in the environment. Keywords: YOLO, MiDaS, perception, localization, decision making

</details>


### [118] [Encoded but Not in Control: Revealing the Grounding Gap in Vision-Language Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06235)
*Shaohan Jiang,Jiahang Cao,Qiduo He,Fengting Deng,Kun Wu,Jingkai Sun,Jiaxu Wang,Qiang Zhang,Qihao Zheng,Chunfeng Song,Ping Luo,Andrew F. Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究语言条件机器人策略中的指令跟随问题，通过场景保持的指令干预实验，揭示VLA策略与世界动作模型在任务成功表象下普遍存在"基于场景推断而非遵循指令"的失败模式，并诊断其成因是指令编码虽存在但未能有效控制动作生成，存在视觉接地的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令跟随是语言条件机器人策略的核心：当同一场景允许多个有效动作时，语言应决定做什么。然而仅凭成功执行无法判断策略是遵循指令还是从场景中推断任务，这一歧义性此前缺乏系统研究。作者希望厘清"任务成功是否等同于指令跟随"以及语言失效的根本原因。

Method: 采用场景保持的指令干预方法：在固定场景下，对指令进行有效目标替换、任意名词替换和无关句子替换。在仿真与真实环境中评估视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略和世界动作模型（WAMs）。并通过逐层动作透镜、线性探针、注意力分析、UMAP与共享非负矩阵分解（NMF）等可解释性工具进行分析。

Result: (a) 当指令要求不同可见物体时，所有被评估策略主要仍接近并抓取与场景关联的原始目标，任务成功并不蕴含指令跟随。(b) 失败并非源于语言不敏感：指令扰动会影响任务表现，逐层动作透镜显示中间动作预测对扰动有响应，线性探针能准确恢复被指示目标，说明修改后的指令已被编码，但很少决定目标选择。(c) 注意力分析表明指令token对动作生成的贡献较弱，目标token注意力可能持续聚焦于原始物体，揭示目标编码与视觉接地之间存在不匹配；UMAP与NMF显示目标信息仍可获取，但表征逐渐被场景身份组织。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了被"表面成功"掩盖的接地鸿沟，并提供了诊断框架。由此确立了一个具体的进展判据：策略应能可靠地跟随用户意图的有效变化，即使这与场景偏好行为相冲突。

Abstract: Instruction following is central to language-conditioned robot policies: language should determine what to do when the same scene permits multiple valid actions. Yet successful execution alone cannot establish whether a policy follows the instruction or infers the task from the scene. We study this ambiguity through scene-preserving instruction interventions, using valid target substitutions, arbitrary nouns, and unrelated sentences while holding the scene fixed. We evaluate vision-language-action (VLA) policies and world-action models (WAMs) in simulation and in real-world experiments. Our analysis addresses three questions: (a) Does task success imply instruction following? When instructions request a different visible object, all evaluated policies predominantly approach and pick up the incorrect original target associated with the scene. (b) Is this failure caused by language insensitivity? Instruction perturbations affect task performance. A layerwise action lens shows intermediate action predictions respond to these perturbations. Linear probes accurately recover instructed targets, indicating modified instructions are encoded despite rarely determining target selection. (c) Why does encoded language fail to control action? Attention analysis indicates weak instruction-token contributions to action generation. Target-token attention can remain focused on the original object, revealing a mismatch between target encoding and visual grounding. UMAP and shared non-negative matrix factorization show target information remains accessible within representations increasingly organized by scene identity. Our findings expose a grounding gap concealed by nominal success and provide a diagnostic framework. They further establish a concrete criterion for progress: policies should reliably follow valid changes in user intent, even when they conflict with scene-favored behavior.

</details>


### [119] [VLA-ZO: Fast Zeroth-Order Adaptation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06271)
*Jaemin Kim,Jiahn Kim,Taesik Gong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-ZO是一个快速零阶适配框架，通过冻结视觉-语言前缀并复用其条件状态，将VLA模型的部署时适配时间大幅降低，同时提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在部署时面临分布偏移，需要适配以保证可靠运行。但传统一阶适配内存开销超过推理平台预算；零阶优化虽内存友好(仅前向)，但梯度估计需大量扰动查询，对大VLA模型速度过慢。因此需要一种快速零阶适配方法。

Method: 利用VLA计算结构：将适配限制在动作侧(action side)，冻结昂贵的视觉-语言前缀，在多个扰动查询和优化器步之间复用其条件状态(conditioning states)；并采用调度感知预取(schedule-aware prefetching)隐藏状态传输开销。

Result: 在LIBERO相机视角偏移任务上，相比基线ZO，q=16时端到端适配时间减少25.59×，q=64时减少32.54×；平均任务成功率从无适配的48.27%提升到58.17%(q=16)和63.58%(q=64)。

Conclusion: 加快ZO可让更大的查询预算变得可行，为在部署平台上实现资源高效的VLA适配提供了有前景的路径。

Abstract: Adapting vision-language-action (VLA) models to deployment-time distribution shifts is important for reliable robotic operation, but conventional first-order adaptation can exceed the memory budget of inference-oriented deployment platforms. Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization offers a forward-only alternative with inference-level memory, but accurate gradient estimation requires many perturbation queries, making naive ZO prohibitively slow for large VLA models. We present VLA-ZO, a framework for fast ZO adaptation that exploits the structure of VLA computation. By confining adaptation to the action side, VLA-ZO keeps the expensive vision-language prefix frozen and reuses its conditioning states across perturbation queries and optimizer steps, while schedule-aware prefetching hides state-transfer overhead. On LIBERO camera-viewpoint shifts, VLA-ZO reduces end-to-end adaptation time by 25.59$\times$ at $q=16$ and 32.54$\times$ at $q=64$ relative to baseline ZO, while improving average task success from 48.27% without adaptation to 58.17% and 63.58%, respectively. These results show that making ZO faster can make larger query budgets practical, providing a promising path toward resource-efficient VLA adaptation on deployment platforms.

</details>


### [120] [Future Anchored Verification and Online Recovery for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06280)
*Zhibin Qin,Zhenxiong Tan,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FAVOR框架，利用WAM预测的未来帧作为锚点进行验证与在线恢复，在不修改策略的前提下提升下游机器人操作任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）先预测任务未来走向再解码动作，但执行一旦偏离预测，后续动作即失效；从已分布外的状态重新规划很少能恢复任务所需，现有执行监控只决定何时停止，不解决"恢复什么"的问题。

Method: FAVOR包含两个组件：Anchor Verifier将每次观测与其锚点（预测帧）及已执行动作比较，标记破坏任务的偏差；Anchor-Guided Recovery用视觉语言模型将被标记的锚点转为简短纠正指令，在增强指令引导下WAM执行该指令以回到预期未来，随后恢复任务。

Result: 在LIBERO上基础WAM成功率由97.85%提升至98.10%，在LIBERO-Plus上由72.60%提升至72.98%，且无需修改策略。

Conclusion: 预测的未来帧本身即可作为验证与恢复的锚点，为WAM提供轻量化的在线纠错机制，在不改动策略的情况下获得一致性提升。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation. They act by first predicting how a task should be performed and then decoding the actions from that future. However, the remaining actions are invalid once execution drifts from the prediction. Simply replanning from the already out of distribution state rarely restores what the task still requires; existing execution monitors decide when to stop, but not what to restore. We observe that the answer is already in hand: the future the WAM predicted before acting depicts exactly the states it intended to pass through. We introduce FAVOR (Future Anchored Verification and Online Recovery), a lightweight framework that keeps these predicted frames as anchors and uses them for verification and recovery. An Anchor Verifier compares each observation with its anchor, together with the executed actions, to flag deviations that break the task. Anchor-Guided Recovery uses a vision-language model to turn the flagged anchor into a short corrective instruction. Under strengthened instruction guidance, the WAM executes this instruction to return to the intended future. It then resumes the task. FAVOR raises the task success of the base WAM from 97.85% to 98.10% on LIBERO and from 72.60% to 72.98% on LIBERO-Plus without modifying the policy.

</details>


### [121] [GAMBIT: Learning to Plan Continuous Multi-Robot Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06290)
*Rishabh Jain,Akmaral Moldagalieva,Lorenzo Magnino,Michael Amir,Keisuke Okumura,Ajay Shankar,Wolfgang Hönig,Amanda Prorok*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GAMBIT 通过模仿学习结合强化学习，在多机器人运动基元层面学习协调启发式策略，并引入带备份轨迹的安全回滚机制，实现超千机器人规模、亚秒级延迟的无碰撞协同，性能超越集中式与分散式基线规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协调中，个体需牺牲局部最优行为以换取团队整体收益（类似国际象棋中的弃子开局 GAMBIT），这类自牺牲式策略难以用人工启发式规则刻画，尤其在密集、强交互环境中。

Method: 面向双积分器连续动力学，将问题设定为运动基元层面的协调选择；先用模仿学习获得协调的运动基元选择策略，再用强化学习进行微调；并引入带备份轨迹的安全回滚机制，保证任意时刻执行均无碰撞。

Result: 相比集中式运动规划器与分散式反应式规划器等基线，GAMBIT 显著更优，且具备良好可扩展性：在连续域中协调超过一千个机器人，规划延迟低于数百毫秒。

Conclusion: 学习式协调启发式结合安全回滚机制，可在密集交互的连续多机器人场景中兼顾团队性能、无碰撞保证与大规模可扩展性。

Abstract: GAMBIT is an opening chess move in which a player sacrifices a piece, typically a pawn, to gain a positional advantage later in the game. Analogously, in multi-robot coordination, individual robots may need to forgo locally reward-maximising behaviours to improve overall team performance. Such self-sacrificial behaviours are difficult to capture with manually designed heuristics, particularly in dense, interaction-rich environments. Focusing on double-integrator continuous dynamics, this work studies how to learn such coordinated heuristics over motion primitives for multi-robot trajectory execution. Our framework, GAMBIT, first learns coordinated motion-primitive selection through imitation learning and subsequently fine-tunes the policy through reinforcement learning. We further introduce a safeguarded rollout mechanism with backup trajectories that guarantees collision-free execution at all times. Experiments demonstrate that GAMBIT substantially outperforms a range of baselines, including centralised motion planners and decentralised reactive planners, while exhibiting strong scalability. In particular, it coordinates over a thousand robots with planning latency below a few hundred milliseconds in continuous domains.

</details>


### [122] [Inspect Robots: Evaluating the Capabilities and Safety of Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06306)
*Christopher Leet,Achu Menon,Sravanthi Machcha,Sabrina Zou,Aayushya Patel,Aditya Kumar Singh,Anish Kr Singh,Galaba Vamsi,Javin Ahuja,Sai Asish Yamani,Tushar Anand,Vedang Alle,Zihan Jack Zhang,Tzu Kit Chan,Jay Chooi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Inspect Robots，一个用于具身智能体评测的模块化开源框架，并基于此评测了六种前沿语言模型策略的能力与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用语言模型对机器人硬件的控制能力日益增强，理解模型具身化后的能力与安全风险对社会影响评估愈发重要，但缺乏系统化的评测工具。

Method: 构建模块化开源框架 Inspect Robots，提供可定制、可复用的物理评测抽象与结果分析组件，并配套自动化评测搭建、执行与终止的基础设施。

Result: 利用该框架对六种基于前沿语言模型的策略进行了能力与安全性评测；发布三个月内获得近10万次下载，早期采用度显著。

Conclusion: Inspect Robots 为具身智能体的标准化评测提供了可用工具，并展现出广泛的社区认可与实用价值。

Abstract: General purpose language models are increasingly able to control robotic hardware. Understanding the capabilities and safety of these models when embodied is therefore increasingly important for understanding their societal impact and risks. To this end, we introduce Inspect Robots, a modular, open-source framework for developing and running evaluations of embodied agents. Inspect Robots pairs customizable, reusable abstractions for specifying physical evaluations and analyzing their results with infrastructure that automates evaluation setup, execution and termination. We demonstrate Inspect Robots by using it to evaluate the capabilities and safety of six policies based on frontier language models. Inspect Robots has seen significant early uptake, receiving nearly 100,000 downloads in the three months since its release.

</details>


### [123] [Dual Variational Autoencoders for Efficient Sim-to-Real Transfer in Low-Cost Robotic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06327)
*Álvaro Díez,Fidel Aznar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出混合迁移学习框架，结合域随机化与特征级域适应，用双卷积VAE共享解码器对齐仿真与真实域潜空间，在真实室内导航任务上达到近91%成功率并可在低成本嵌入式平台部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本机器人的视觉自主导航面临仿真训练环境与真实运行条件之间的显著差异（sim-to-real gap）：直接从仿真迁移策略往往失效，而仅用真实数据训练又不现实。因此需要一个能有效桥接该差距且适配资源受限平台的导航方案。

Method: 提出混合迁移学习框架，将域随机化与特征级域适应相结合。采用双卷变分自编码器（dual convolutional VAE）架构并共享解码器，在45225张仿真图像与仅4556张真实样本上训练，学习紧凑的公共潜表示空间以对齐两个域的分布；并引入两种互补的数据增强技术扩充有限的真实数据。

Result: 在真实室内导航的图像分类任务上平均成功率达约91%，显著优于仅仿真训练与仅真实数据训练的基线；通过真实世界部署验证，策略成功引导低成本机器人完成反应式探索任务；计算量估计确认其适用于Raspberry Pi 4与NVIDIA Jetson Nano等资源受限嵌入式平台。

Conclusion: 该工作为低成本机器人系统提供了一种实用、高效的导航策略开发方案，证明结合域随机化与特征级域适应的混合迁移学习可有效桥接sim-to-real差距。

Abstract: Vision-based autonomous navigation for low-cost robots remains a fundamental challenge, primarily due to the significant gap between simulated training environments and real-world operational conditions. Direct policy transfer from simulation is often ineffective, while training exclusively on real data is impractical. We propose a hybrid transfer learning framework that effectively bridges the sim-to-real gap by combining domain randomization with feature-level domain adaptation. Our method employs a dual convolutional variational autoencoder architecture with a shared decoder, trained on an extensive set of 45225 simulated images and a minimal set of only 4556 real-world samples. This architecture learns a compact, common latent representation space that aligns the distributions of both domains. The adaptation process is further enhanced by two complementary data augmentation techniques designed to expand the limited real-world data. Experimental evaluation demonstrates that our method achieves an average success rate of almost 91% on image classification tasks for real-world indoor navigation, significantly outperforming both simulation-only and real-world-only training. We validate these findings through a direct, real-world deployment, where the proposed policy successfully guides a low-cost robot in a reactive exploration task. Furthermore, we validate the model's efficiency through a rigorous computational estimation, confirming its suitability for resource-constrained embedded platforms such as the Raspberry Pi 4 and NVIDIA Jetson Nano. This work presents a practical solution for developing effective and efficient navigation policies for low-cost robotic systems.

</details>


### [124] [DexForge: High-Fidelity Physics-Informed Dexterous Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06331)
*Meizhong Wang,Kun Cao,Ruiqi Ni,Lihua Xie,Yiguang Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexForge：可微分物理框架，将人类视频演示转换为高保真机器人灵巧手轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示蕴含丰富的精细灵巧操作样本，是机器人训练数据的理想来源；但受物理约束限制，跨本体高保真复现演示动作及手-物交互仍具挑战。

Method: 1) 从视觉观测重建球形高斯（spherical-Gaussian）物体模型与手-物运动；2) 构建可微分仿真器，将高效高斯碰撞检测与现有可微分动力学结合；3) 基于该仿真器提出接触感知的运动学重定向与力感知的动力学重定向，先由机器人适配的稳定接触指导运动学参考构建，再进行基于梯度的控制细化以精确复现物理运动。

Result: 在130个DexYCB与HOT3D演示、七种灵巧手上，成功率较基线提升约35–53个百分点；成功轨迹的物体位置与姿态跟踪误差分别降低约34–67%与71–78%。进一步实验验证了向MuJoCo的开环迁移及真机执行。

Conclusion: DexForge通过可微分物理与两级重定向，实现跨本体高保真手-物交互复现，并具备仿真到真机的迁移能力。

Abstract: Human demonstrations offer rich examples of precise dexterous manipulation and a promising source of robot training data. However, high-fidelity reproduction of demonstrated motions and hand-object interactions across robot embodiments remains challenging under physical constraints. We present DexForge, a differentiable physics-grounded framework for converting human video demonstrations into high-fidelity robot trajectories. We reconstruct spherical-Gaussian object models and hand-object motion from visual observations, then build a differentiable simulator combining efficient Gaussian collision detection with existing differentiable dynamics. Based on this simulator, DexForge combines contact-aware kinematic retargeting with force-aware dynamics retargeting: robot-adapted stable contacts guide kinematic reference construction and subsequent gradient-based control refinement for precise physical motion reproduction. Experiments on 130 DexYCB and HOT3D demonstrations across seven dexterous hands show success-rate gains of approximately 35-53 percentage points over the baseline, with object position and orientation tracking errors on successful trajectories reduced by approximately 34-67% and 71-78%, respectively. Further experiments demonstrate open-loop transfer to MuJoCo and real-robot execution. Our project page is available at https://wmz1226.github.io/DexForge/

</details>


### [125] [Towards Robust Prehensile Manipulation in Open-Ended Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06376)
*Mathilde Kappel,Mahdi Khoramshahi,Louis Annabi,Faïz Ben Amar,Stéphane Doncieux*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用质量-多样性（QD）算法解决开放环境下的机器人抓持式操作任务，通过高效发现大量鲁棒抓取构型，将其作为生成多样化操作轨迹的可靠起点，从而提升泛化性与适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放、持续演化的环境中，机器人抓持式操作需要大量鲁棒且多样的抓取与轨迹配置。传统方法难以高效发现这种多样性，导致泛化能力和适应性不足，难以支撑实际部署。

Method: 采用质量-多样性（QD）算法，高效发掘广泛且鲁棒的抓取构型；将这些构型作为可靠初始点，用于在关节物体上生成多样化的抓持式操作轨迹。

Result: 发现了广泛且鲁棒的抓取配置，并基于此生成了多样化的操作轨迹，操作行为的多样性得到提升。

Conclusion: 操作行为的多样性增强了泛化能力与适应性，使系统能够在持续演化的开放世界环境中有效部署。

Abstract: We propose to use Quality-Diversity (QD) algorithms to solve robotic prehensile manipulation tasks in open-ended environments. Our approach enables the efficient discovery of a wide range robust grasp configurations, which serve as reliable starting points for generating diverse prehensile manipulation trajectories on articulated objects. The resulting diversity in manipulation behaviors enhances generalization and adaptability, enabling effective deployment continuously evolving open-world settings.

</details>


### [126] [Visual Swarm Navigation via Deep Reinforcement Learning and Evolutionary Hybrid Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06400)
*Álvaro Díez,Fidel Aznar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合多智能体强化学习与神经进化策略（交叉熵方法、协方差矩阵自适应进化策略）的混合方法，自动合成仅依赖单目相机的低成本集群机器人导航控制器，在仿真中实现了鲁棒可扩展的集体探索，性能媲美更昂贵的距离传感器方法且平均能耗降低31.40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂动态环境（如搜救、环境监测）中的先进自动化需要稳健且高性价比的方案，集群机器人是理想范式。其核心难题在于以数据驱动方式设计去中心化控制器，以产生涌现式集体行为。同时，实际部署要求控制器能在低成本、资源受限平台上运行并保持计算与能耗效率。

Method: 提出AI驱动的混合方法：将多智能体强化学习与神经进化策略协同结合，具体采用交叉熵方法（CEM）与协方差矩阵自适应进化策略（CMA-ES）来优化预训练的单体导航策略。底层采用紧凑神经网络，仅依赖单目相机图像，针对低功耗资源受限平台进行架构精简。实验在高保真物理仿真器中完成。

Result: 所得控制器可实现多种室内环境中稳健且可扩展的集体探索。基于CEM训练的控制器探索覆盖率更优，比CMA-ES多访问36.20%的区域。最佳视觉策略的探索性能与传统依赖更昂贵距离传感器的方法在统计上相当，同时平均能耗降低31.40%。

Conclusion: 研究验证了一套有效且经济可行的自主控制系统，表明仅凭单目视觉即可在低成本集群平台上实现高效集体智能，为在真实工程应用中部署高能效集群控制提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Swarm robotics presents a robust and cost-effective paradigm for advanced automation in complex, dynamic environments, such as those encountered in search and rescue or environmental monitoring. A fundamental challenge for this field is the data-driven design of decentralized controllers capable of generating emergent collective behaviors. This paper proposes a novel, AI-driven hybrid methodology for the automatic synthesis of swarm robotic controllers for autonomous visual navigation. This approach synergistically combines multi-agent reinforcement learning with neuro-evolutionary strategies, specifically leveraging implementations of the cross-entropy method and the covariance matrix adaptation evolution strategy to optimize a pre-trained individual navigation policy. The underlying deep architecture is engineered for low-cost, resource-constrained platforms, utilizing a compact neural network that relies exclusively on monocular camera imagery. This vision-based design emphasizes computational and energy efficiency, a critical requirement for practical swarm deployments. Experiments, performed in a high-fidelity physics simulator, demonstrate that the resulting controllers enable robust and scalable collective exploration of diverse indoor environments. The controller trained using our cross-entropy method achieves superior exploration coverage, visiting 36.20% more regions compared to the covariance matrix adaptation evolution strategy. Critically, our best vision-based policy achieves exploration performance statistically comparable to traditional methods relying on more expensive distance sensors, while delivering a significant 31.40% average reduction in energy consumption. These findings validate an effective and economically viable autonomous control system, establishing a path for deploying highly efficient collective intelligence in real-world engineering applications.

</details>


### [127] [A Taxonomy on Collective Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06427)
*Guillermo GP-Lenza,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Martín Molina,Ricardo Sanz,Pascual Campoy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文辨析多机器人系统中MA、SSA、TSA三个概念，指出它们构成包含层级，并提出二维分类法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人团队在动态非结构化环境中执行复杂任务时，需对自身、环境和任务状态保持准确一致的认知表示；但文献中Mutual Awareness、Shared Situational Awareness和Team Situational Awareness常被混用，掩盖了它们对协调的不同要求。

Method: 提出二维分类法，沿"认知对象"和"认知分布"两个维度区分三类概念；以其构成包含层级和从个体化理解到统一理解的分布谱系来刻画；并以异构空中与地面机器人的搜救案例将各分类位置落实到具体协调需求。

Result: 三类概念并非同义：MA、SSA、TSA构成作用范围逐级包含的层级，MA为完全个体化理解，SSA为完全统一理解，TSA处于混合位置；二维分类法能明确各概念对应的协调需求。

Conclusion: 将MA、SSA、TSA视为同义词会模糊其各自的协调含义；该分类框架为多机器人系统集体认知的规范定义、设计比较提供了概念基础。

Abstract: As robotic teams tackle increasingly complex tasks in dynamic and unstructured environments, effective coordination requires agents to maintain accurate, aligned representations of their environment, teammates, and mission state. We argue that Mutual Awareness, Shared Situational Awareness, and Team Situational Awareness --- three concepts widely invoked in the multi-robot systems literature --- are not interchangeable: they form a containment hierarchy in which each type subsumes the previous in scope, and span a distribution spectrum from fully individualized understanding (MA) to fully uniform understanding (SSA), with TSA occupying a mixed position. We establish this through a two-dimensional taxonomy organized along the object of awareness and awareness distribution axes. Treating these terms as synonyms obscures the precise coordination requirements each imposes on a robotic system. A Search and Rescue case study with heterogeneous aerial and ground robots grounds each taxonomy position in concrete coordination requirements. These findings provide a conceptual foundation for principled specification, design, and comparison of collective awareness in multi-robot systems.

</details>


### [128] [Odyssey: A Closed-Loop Benchmark for Long-Horizon Real-World Driving with Explicit Navigation Routes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06469)
*Jungho Kim,Hongjae Shin,Seunghoon Yu,Heecheol Yoo,Myeongjun Kim,Jiyong Oh,Donghyuk Kwak,Seunghyeop Nam,Haesung Oh,Hyunju Kim,Hyungchan Cho,Jaehyun Park,Soo Won Seo,Jun Won Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出闭环长时程驾驶基准 Odyssey，用100秒nuPlan日志重建100个场景，以SD地图路线替代方向指令，引入路线合规与换道准备指标及RouteDS评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端驾驶基准仅评估短片段，无法揭示早期决策的后续影响；且模糊的方向指令使导航目标不明确，难以衡量规划器是否真正遵循意图导航。

Method: 构建 Odyssey 闭环基准：100个场景各由100秒 nuPlan 驾驶日志重建，保留导航操作与交通交互上下文；用显式SD地图路线（指定行驶道路）替代方向指令，局部驾驶动作由传感器规划决定；rollout中对3DGS渲染图像做扩散式精修以减少沿自车轨迹的渲染伪影。新增 SD Route Compliance 与 Pre-Lane Change Score 两项指标，并提出 RouteDS（在 Driving Score 上加入SD路线偏离与换道准备失败的惩罚）。适配包括VLA在内的SOTA规划器进行评估。

Result: 在适配的SOTA规划器（含VLA模型）上，用所提指标评估其导航表现；Odyssey揭示了端到端驾驶中路线表示与集成方面的开放问题。

Conclusion: 长时程闭环评估对端到端驾驶至关重要；以SD路线作为一致导航目标并结合相应指标与评分，能更有效考察规划器的路线遵循与前瞻性准备能力。基准代码与适配基线将公开。

Abstract: Closed-loop evaluation of end-to-end driving requires continuous rollouts that reveal how earlier decisions affect subsequent driving. However, existing benchmarks evaluate only short segments and fail to capture later consequences. Ambiguous directional commands also obscure the intended navigation objective. We introduce Odyssey, a closed-loop benchmark for long-horizon driving comprising 100 scenarios, each reconstructed from a 100-second nuPlan driving log to preserve the context of navigation maneuvers and traffic interactions. To provide a consistent navigation objective, Odyssey replaces directional commands with explicit standard-definition (SD) map routes that specify which roads to follow, while sensor-based planning determines local driving actions. Throughout these rollouts, diffusion-based refinement of 3DGS-rendered images reduces rendering artifacts along the ego trajectory. To assess how effectively planners follow these routes and prepare for upcoming maneuvers, we introduce SD Route Compliance and Pre-Lane Change Score. These assessments are complemented by RouteDS, which extends the Driving Score with penalties for SD-route deviations and failed lane preparation. We adapt state-of-the-art planners, including vision-language-action (VLA) models, and evaluate their navigation performance using these metrics. Odyssey highlights open questions in route representation and integration for E2E driving. Benchmark code and adapted baselines will be released publicly.

</details>


### [129] [Traversability-Aware Cooperative Path Planning for Human-UGV Casualty Evacuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06487)
*Kristian Dalland,Prithvi Poddar,Souma Chowdhury,Karthik Dantu,Ehsan T. Esfahani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究面向人-UGV双智能体搜救伤员转运任务，将人建模为具有代谢能耗与疲劳约束的物理协作者，通过优化切换点实现联合路径规划，在节省时间的同时显著降低人体能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多机器人路径规划虽研究充分，但将所有智能体视为机器人（机械代价模型、传感器感知的可通行性），在人-机器人协作中人类仅被当作指挥或监督者，未被建模为具有独特移动约束与动态能量储备的物理协同导航者。因此需要研究人与UGV的联合路径规划。

Method: 针对双智能体人-UGV团队的搜救伤员转运场景，人类采用Pandolf-Santee代谢代价模型并结合疲劳调制速度，UGV采用滚动阻力能量模型与地形相关速度限制；利用两者互补的可通行性（人能穿越茂密植被和浅水，UGV在开阔地与道路速度更优），优化称为切换点的伤员交接位置以最小化总任务时间。

Result: 在多个含程序化生成高程与土地覆盖数据的合成1km²环境中评估：优化策略较人类单独基线平均任务时间减少5.3%，较未优化切换点的朴素人-UGV策略减少7.0%，人体能耗较基线降低17.8%。朴素策略虽使人体能耗降幅更大（22.4%），但任务时间较基线增加2%。

Conclusion: 切换点优化是实现人-UGV协作时间收益的必要条件；仅依靠分工而不优化交接位置，可能以任务时间增加为代价换取能耗下降。

Abstract: Heterogeneous multi-robot path planning is a well-studied problem in which agents with disparate kinematic and dynamic models must coordinate to achieve shared objectives. These formulations, however, treat all agents as robotic-their cost models are mechanical and their traversability is sensor-derived. In human-robot teaming, the human partner remains relegated to command and supervisory roles rather than being modeled as a physical co-navigator with distinct mobility constraints and dynamic energy reserves. This work investigates joint path planning for a two-agent human-UGV team in search-and-rescue casualty retrieval scenarios. We model the human agent using the Pandolf-Santee metabolic cost model with fatigue-modulated speed, and the UGV using a rolling-resistance energy model with terrain-dependent speed limits. By exploiting the complementary traversability of each agent-the human's ability to traverse dense vegetation and shallow water versus the UGV's superior speed on open terrain and roads-we optimize casualty transfer locations, termed switch points, to minimize total mission time. Evaluated across multiple synthetic 1km2 environments with procedurally generated elevation and land-cover data, the optimized strategy reduces mean mission time by 5.3% relative to a human-only baseline and by 7.0% relative to a naive human-UGV strategy without switch point optimization, while reducing human energy expenditure by 17.8% relative to baseline. Notably, the naive strategy reduces human energy expenditure by a larger margin (22.4%) but incurs a 2% increase in mission time relative to baseline, illustrating that switch point optimization is necessary to realize time savings from human-UGV teaming.

</details>


### [130] [MarvisNav: Making Memory Visible on Route Choices for Zero-Shot Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06510)
*Jincheng Wang,Chi Pui Chan,Wei Zeng,Shuyang Zhang,Jianhao Jiao,Dimitrios Kanoulas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MarvisNav将探索记忆直接可视化为视觉路径候选，让VLM联合评估目标相关性与探索状态，无需策略训练即达ZSON最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ZSON方法中，VLM仅从自我中心图像推断搜索区域，而探索历史被单独表示为文本或地图，导致记忆与路径选择之间的对应关系需要额外融合步骤或被隐式恢复，割裂了目标相关性与已探索区域的信息。

Method: 提出MarvisNav框架，维护拓扑图，并将候选节点及其探索状态投影到自我中心视图上，形成"承载记忆的视觉路径候选"。探索状态刻画局部探索进度，超越二元访问标记；通过将探索状态直接绑定到每个视觉候选，使VLM可在一次评估中联合判断目标相关性与探索状态，无需事后融合或重排序。

Result: 无需策略训练，在HM3D上达到81.2% SR和42.5% SPL的SOTA，在MP3D上保持竞争力；VLM调用量远低于代表性VLM方法（如仅WMNav的7.5%）；真实机器人实验在多样场景中验证了实用性。

Conclusion: 记忆表示方式塑造VLM决策与ZSON性能，有效的记忆利用不仅取决于记忆是否可用，还取决于其如何被表示。

Abstract: When searching for an object, people choose their next move by considering both likely target locations and places already explored. The current view can cue place-associated memories, bringing target relevance and prior exploration into the same spatial context. In many zero-shot object navigation (ZSON) methods, however, vision-language models (VLMs) infer promising search areas from egocentric images, while exploration history is represented separately, e.g., as text or maps. This separation either requires an additional fusion step or leaves the correspondence between memory and route choices implicit for the VLM to recover. We instead make exploration memory directly visible on visual route choices. We propose MarvisNav, a ZSON framework that maintains a topological graph and projects candidate nodes together with their exploration states onto egocentric views as memory-bearing visual route choices. These states capture local exploration progress beyond binary visitation. By binding exploration state directly to each visual candidate, MarvisNav enables the VLM to jointly evaluate target relevance and exploration state without a separate post-hoc fusion or reranking stage. Without policy training, MarvisNav achieves state-of-the-art performance on HM3D (81.2% SR and 42.5% SPL), while remaining competitive on MP3D. It also outperforms representative VLM-based methods with far fewer VLM calls (e.g., 7.5% of WMNav). Real-robot experiments across diverse scenes further validate its practical deployability. Beyond MarvisNav, our study shows that memory representation shapes VLM decisions and ZSON performance, highlighting that effective memory use depends not only on its availability, but also on how it is represented. Code and project page will be available at \url{https://wangjincheng1998.github.io/MarvisNav/}.

</details>


### [131] [ArtifactArena: Evaluating Models by What They Build in the Physical World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06511)
*Kushagra Tiwary*,David Mayo*,Nikhil Behari,Xiangzhou Sun,Abdulrahman Alabdulkareem,Isaac Galatzer-Levy,Boris Katz,Brian Cheung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ArtifactArena：一个开放式的物理落地硬件-软件协同设计挑战平台，通过让模型设计可参赛机器人并以 Elo 锦标赛排名来评估前沿模型的物理工程能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估前沿模型不应只看其"说了什么"，而应看其在真实物理环境中"能工程化构建什么"。现有评测缺乏对开放式物理设计能力的持续、非饱和衡量手段。

Method: 构建开放平台 ArtifactArena，任务为设计在模拟竞技场中竞赛的全功能机器人；设置三种测试框架分别考察零样本能力、基于验证器的迭代精炼、以及开放式物理设计；利用文本描述、物理模拟器反馈和游戏对局数据对机器人作品进行精炼；并通过作品间的一对一锦标赛以 Elo 排名评估前沿模型。

Result: 以 Elo 排序形式给出前沿模型在物理设计任务上的能力基准，并开源框架与锦标赛基础设施以支持社区持续提交。

Conclusion: 建立了一个"活的"、非饱和的测试平台，可持续衡量开放式智能在物理世界的扩展极限，避免基准快速饱和。

Abstract: To evaluate the frontier, we must measure models not by what they say, but by what they can engineer and build in grounded physical environments. We introduce \textsc{ArtifactArena}, an open-ended platform where models face a physically grounded hardware-software co-design challenge: engineering fully functional robots to compete in a simulated arena. We evaluate a frontier model's zero-shot, verifier guided refinement, and open-ended physical design capabilities through three harnesses that refine their bot artifacts based on text descriptions, physics simulator feedback, and gameplay data. We benchmark these capabilities with an Elo ranking of frontier models derived from head-to-head tournaments between their artifacts. By releasing this framework and tournament infrastructure for ongoing community submissions, we establish a living, non-saturating testbed to continuously measure the expanding limits of open-ended intelligence in the physical world. Please visit \href{https://artifactarena.ai}{https://artifactarena.ai} for more information.

</details>


### [132] [SimForcing: Distilling Simulation Motion Priors into Real-Domain Robot World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06598)
*Xiaodong Wang,Tianle Li,Chuanxin Song,Junliang Xie,Zhanmi Zhong,Suiying Wu,Peixi Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SimForcing：以仿真为运动知识源与可控参考，联合训练机器人世界模型，在无外部具身预训练下取得最优生成指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件机器人世界模型需既精确响应机器人轨迹，又保持逼真视觉动态，但从异构机器人视频中同时学到两者十分困难。仿真能提供结构化运动监督，却存在外观差异导致的直接迁移障碍，且仿真预测不准确时可能误导真实视频生成。

Method: 提出 SimForcing 仿真引导框架，将仿真同时用作可迁移运动知识来源和可控预测参考。其一，通过潜空间运动蒸馏，从仿真教师对齐潜空间中的时序变化，内化运动先验并抑制外观差异影响；其二，引入带条件丢弃的多块仿真条件，利用预测的仿真轨迹而不对其精度过度依赖。仿真条件无分类器引导（CFG）统一上述两点，在基于内化运动知识的预测与额外由仿真潜变量引导的预测之间取得平衡。联合训练的学生模型同时生成仿真条件和真实域视频，推理时无需额外世界模型。

Result: 在 Bridge 数据集上，无需外部具身预训练即取得最优 PSNR、SSIM、LPIPS 与 FVD。在 InternData-A1 上的评测进一步验证其对不同机器人数据集的适用性。此外，用训练好的世界模型初始化视觉-语言-动作模型，提升了 LIBERO 成功率，表明其对下游策略学习有价值。

Conclusion: SimForcing 通过潜空间运动蒸馏与仿真条件 CFG 有效结合仿真运动监督与真实视频生成，兼顾生成质量与动作响应，并可迁移至其他机器人数据集及下游策略学习。

Abstract: Action-conditioned robot world models must respond precisely to robot trajectories while preserving realistic visual dynamics, yet learning both from heterogeneous robot videos remains challenging. Simulation offers structured motion supervision, but appearance differences hinder direct transfer, and inaccurate simulation predictions can misguide real-video generation. We present SimForcing, a simulation-guided framework that uses simulation both as a source of transferable motion knowledge and as a controllable reference for prediction. First, we transfer motion knowledge from a simulation teacher through latent-motion distillation, aligning temporal changes in latent space to internalize motion priors while mitigating the influence of appearance differences. Second, we introduce multi-block simulation conditioning with condition dropout to exploit predicted simulation trajectories without relying excessively on their accuracy. Our simulation-conditioning classifier-free guidance scheme unifies these two ideas by balancing predictions based on internalized motion knowledge with those additionally guided by simulation latents. The jointly trained student generates both simulation conditions and real-domain videos, requiring no additional world model at inference. On Bridge, SimForcing achieves the best PSNR, SSIM, LPIPS, and FVD among the compared methods without external embodied pretraining. Evaluation on InternData-A1 further supports its applicability across robot datasets. Moreover, using our trained world model to initialize a vision-language-action model improves LIBERO success, suggesting its utility for downstream policy learning. \url{https://github.com/Wang-Xiaodong1899/SimForcing}

</details>


### [133] [Physics Residual Dynamics and Reduced Order Whole-Body Planning for Obstacle Aware Human Robot Cloth CoTransportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06641)
*Moein Forouhar,Kosar Behnia,Anirvan Dutta,Hamid Sadeghian,Ville Kyrki,Sami Haddadin,Gokhan Alcan,Eckehard Steinbach*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层规划框架，结合学习布料模型与双臂移动机械臂降阶全身模型，实现柔性物体人机协同搬运中的变形预测与避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性物体人机协同搬运需在运动中预测物体变形，因为避障间隙同时取决于抓取点与不可驱动的物体内部状态，而现有方法难以兼顾预测精度与计算效率。

Method: 提出物理残差条件循环变分自编码器（p-cRVAE），通过学习对高效线性化物理模型的残差修正，从抓取点观测预测完整布料构型；将预测动力学嵌入基于双臂移动机械臂降阶表示的MPPI规划器，保留非完整基座约束与臂工作空间限制；再由MPC层细化采样运动为平滑可执行参考，用于全身控制。

Result: 降阶模型跟踪性能与完整17自由度模型相当，计算时间减少约80%；在四种协同搬运场景和两种搬运速度下，框架保持布料-障碍间隙，而角点跟随基线发生碰撞；全身细化将最终布料变形从0.93 m降至0.28 m。

Conclusion: 该分层框架通过在40步规划时域内限制误差累积，有效实现柔性物体协同搬运的变形预测与避障，同时显著降低计算成本并改善控制执行质量。

Abstract: Human--robot co-transportation of deformable objects requires predicting object deformation during motion, since obstacle clearance depends on both the grasp points and the unactuated interior. We present a hierarchical planning framework that combines a learned cloth model with a reduced-order whole-body model of a dual-arm mobile manipulator. A physics-residual conditional recurrent variational autoencoder (p-cRVAE) predicts the full cloth configuration from grasp-point observations by learning a residual correction to a computationally efficient linearized physics model, limiting error accumulation over 40-step planning horizon. The predicted cloth dynamics are embedded in a model predictive path integral (MPPI) planner using a reduced-order representation of a dual-arm mobile manipulator that preserves the non-holonomic base constraint and arm workspace limits. An MPC layer subsequently refines the sampled motion into smooth, executable references for whole-body control. The reduced-order formulation achieves tracking performance comparable to the full 17-DoF model while reducing computation time by approximately 80%. Across four co-transportation scenarios and two carrying speeds, the proposed framework maintains cloth-obstacle clearance where a corner-following baseline results in collisions, while whole-body refinement reduces final cloth deformation from 0.93\,m to 0.28\,m.

</details>


### [134] [AffordCraft: Scalable Construction of Task-Ready Simulation Assets from Single Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06643)
*Haoyun Yang,Xueyang Zhou,Ziyi Xie,Yongchao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AffordCraft 通过"检索而非生成"的方式，从单张 RGB 图像与任务指令中构建可模拟的关节化物体资产：定位物体与可操作部件，从关节资产库中选取匹配条目并拟合到图像，同时保持部件与关节完整，从而在无框/掩码条件下生成物理有效的资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人仿真学习依赖模拟器提供的物体，许多任务需要具备独立部件、可实现特定运动的关节以及接触下仍成立的物理属性的物体。现有方法对每张图像重新恢复这种结构：生成模型预测的部件与关节大多无法在仿真中稳定放置或运动，而通用智能体则需为每张照片进行长时间的多次模型调用，成本高且成功率低。因此需要一种更高效、物理有效的资产生成范式。

Method: AffordCraft 采用检索而非生成的思路：给定单张 RGB 图像和任务指令，先定位目标物体及待操作部件，再从关节化资产库中检索匹配条目，并将其拟合到图像上，同时保持其部件与关节结构完整。该方法不依赖标注物体框或掩码，且库规模扩大无需修改方法本身。

Result: 在来自31个类别的2,000张照片中，AffordCraft 为1,703张生成了物理有效资产；五种生成方法在同一批照片上通过率最高仅45%，且每个有效资产所需GPU时间中位数是本文方法的10至78倍。在50张杂乱图像中，自动检测后237个标注物体中有162个通过相同的物理测试。资产库从141增至11,372条时，类别覆盖率由46%升至100%，带有请求标签的选取比例由18%升至51%。作者还基于构建的资产生成操作任务（单物体与组合场景），在脚本演示上训练的策略能从训练中未见的初始状态完成两类任务。

Conclusion: 以检索为核心的 AffordCraft 能高效地从单张图像构建物理有效、结构完整的关节化物体资产，显著优于生成式方法，且可通过扩充资产库无缝提升覆盖与标签匹配率，并支持下游操作任务策略学习。

Abstract: Robot learning in simulation depends on the objects the simulator offers. Many tasks need objects with separate parts, joints that allow the required motion, and physical properties that remain valid under contact. Existing methods recover this structure anew for every image: generative models predict parts and joints that mostly fail to settle or move in simulation, and general-purpose agents need a long session of model calls for each photograph. AffordCraft builds such an asset from a single RGB image and a task instruction by retrieval instead of generation: it locates the object and the part to operate, selects a matching entry from a library of articulated assets, and fits it to the image while keeping its parts and joints intact. Without any box or mask marking the object, AffordCraft produces a physically valid asset for 1,703 of 2,000 photographs from 31 categories. Five generative methods pass on at most 45% of the same photographs and, at the median, need 10 to 78 times our GPU time per valid asset. On 50 cluttered images, 162 of 237 annotated objects pass the same physical test after automatic detection. Growing the library from 141 to 11,372 entries needs no change to the method and raises category coverage from 46% to 100% and the share of selections with the requested label from 18% to 51%. We also build manipulation tasks from the constructed assets, both with single objects and in composed scenes; policies trained on scripted demonstrations complete both kinds of tasks from initial states unseen in training.

</details>


### [135] [Recursive Video In-Context Learning for Agentic Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06843)
*Wenrui Bao,Xinxin Liu,Bingxin Xu,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 RV-ICL，一种免训练方法，将示范视频构建为可分层次导航的结构，使 LLM agent 能按需读取从关键帧到短片段的不同粒度信息，从而提升 VLA 策略执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM agent 依赖文本记忆改进跨情节表现，但文本记忆只记录"做了什么"而非"如何做"。示范视频虽能展示操作过程，却难以直接放入 agent 上下文：完整视频拖慢每轮推理，固定关键帧丢失决定抓取成败的接触细节，且 agent 所需信息随任务阶段变化（规划时需任务结构，执行接触时需局部帧）。

Method: RV-ICL 将示范视频转化为按子事件（如抓取、释放）划分的层次结构，层级由粗到细：全任务关键帧→阶段→关键时刻→短片段，并通过只读工具暴露给 agent。agent 在规划前读取粗粒度层级；执行中当某步骤需要更多细节时重新进入层级，仅加载当前子目标的片段。每个任务仅需一个示范，无需训练。

Result: 基于 RPent 构建，RV-ICL 将 LIBERO-PRO 成功率从 92.6% 提升至 96.5%，将 LIBERO-Plus 成功率从 86.7% 提升至 95.8%。

Conclusion: 将示范视频组织为可导航的层次结构并按需检索，比将视频作为一次性提示或固定关键帧更有效，能在免训练条件下显著提升 VLA 策略执行成功率。

Abstract: LLM agents that orchestrate frozen vision-language-action (VLA) policies improve across episodes through text memory, which records what the agent did but not how the task is done. A demonstration video shows it, but fits poorly into an agent's context. The full video slows every turn, fixed keyframes lose the contact detail that decides whether a grasp holds, and what the agent needs shifts from the task's structure while planning to the frames around each contact. We introduce Recursive Video In-Context Learning (RV-ICL), a training-free method that turns a demonstration into a hierarchy the agent navigates rather than a prompt it receives. The hierarchy is built from the sub-events of the demonstration, such as grasps and releases. Its levels grow finer, from keyframes of the whole task to phases, moments and short clips, and are exposed through read-only tools. The agent reads the coarse levels before planning. During execution it re-enters the hierarchy whenever a step needs more detail and loads only the clip of its current sub-goal. One demonstration per task is enough. Built on RPent, RV-ICL raises success from 92.6% to 96.5% on LIBERO-PRO and from 86.7% to 95.8% on LIBERO-Plus.

</details>


### [136] [InterMimicGen: Scaling Humanoid Loco-Manipulation through Self-Evolving Motion Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06850)
*Yucheng Zhang,Sirui Xu,Jinhong Li,Liuyu Bian,Anatulya Nandi,Derek Zhang,Xiangchen Liu,Xueting Li,Umar Iqbal,Yu-Xiong Wang,Liang-Yan Gui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出InterMimicGen自演化运动模仿框架，将异构人类-物体交互演示重定向为人形机器人参考，通过物理仿真通才跟踪器与数据飞轮相互促进，持续扩展可执行灵巧全身运动数据，并成功迁移至真实机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类-物体交互捕捉数据虽富含监督信息，但存在稀疏、异构、且无法被机器人直接执行的问题；现有的人形运动跟踪系统在灵巧全身移动操作上的规模与多样性不足，缺乏从人类演示到机器人可执行运动资源的统一路径。

Method: 1) 整合动作捕捉的人类-物体交互数据集，在保留全身协调性与灵巧手-物关系的前提下重定向为人形机器人参考，构建大规模多样化的参考集合；2) 在带灵巧手的仿真人形机器人上训练基于物理的通才跟踪器，执行上述参考；3) 构建数据飞轮：每轮对交互发生位置与身体执行方式进行保持任务的微小改动，微调跟踪器，仅保留仿真执行能完成任务的变体作为下一轮种子，迭代累积扩展覆盖范围。

Result: 实验表明：跨机器人构型实现保持接触的重定向；单一通才策略实现广泛跟踪；可执行运动随增强轮次持续增长；并成功迁移到真实机器人。

Conclusion: InterMimicGen为从异构人类演示到持续扩展的人形机器人运动资源提供了一条统一路径，验证了机器人运动数据与跟踪策略相互促进的自演化范式的有效性。

Abstract: Captured human-object interactions provide rich supervision for humanoid loco-manipulation, but they are sparse, heterogeneous, and not directly executable by robots. We introduce InterMimicGen, a self-evolving motion-imitation framework in which robot motion data and a tracking policy improve each other. First, we consolidate motion-captured human-object interaction datasets and retarget them into humanoid robot references while preserving whole-body coordination and dexterous hand-object relationships. This produces a large and diverse humanoid robot reference collection for dexterous whole-body loco-manipulation. Second, we train a physics-based generalist tracker that executes these references in simulation on a humanoid with dexterous hands, covering a scale and diversity beyond prior humanoid tracking systems for loco-manipulation. Third, we close a data flywheel: each round makes small, task-preserving changes to where an interaction takes place and how the body performs it, fine-tunes the tracker on them, and keeps only the variants whose simulated execution completes the task, which seed the next round. With more iterations, these small edits compound into broader coverage around the sparse original demonstrations while preserving task semantics and motion quality. Experiments show contact-preserving retargeting across robot configurations, broad tracking with a single generalist policy, executable motions that keep growing over augmentation rounds, and transfer to real robots. InterMimicGen provides a unified path from heterogeneous human demonstrations to a continually expanding motion resource for humanoid robot learning.

</details>
